# AI-slop audit — full monorepo (excluding `example/**`)

**Date:** 2026-09-11 · **Commit:** `bdac6c646` · **Branch:** `dev` · **Mode:** read-only, flag-for-rework only (no code was changed).

## 0. TL;DR

- **69 systemic patterns** (61 P1 / 8 P2) and **615 individual findings** (27 P1 / 276 P2 / 312 P3) across 13 slices, every one citing a `path:line` that an auditor opened and an independent verifier re-opened.
- **The repo's own rules are the rubric, and the rules are losing.** Root `AGENTS.md` caps comment blocks at 5 lines, forbids history narration, `interface`, `globalStyle`, `as any`; the codebase has hundreds of each. `bun run lint:comments` gates only `.kt`/`.swift` (`scripts/check-comments.ts:68` accepts `.ts`/`.tsx`, but nothing invokes it that way), which is exactly where the comment debt has moved.
- **Five themes account for ~80 % of the weight** — in order of leverage: (1) comment prose over budget / narrating history and mechanism (18 patterns, 118 findings, every TS slice); (2) copy-paste duplication where one parameter differs (17 patterns, 77 findings); (3) tests that cannot fail — tautologies, mock-only asserts, framework re-tests (10 patterns, 64 findings); (4) documentation that describes a repo that no longer exists — all four agent-instruction trees, most `AGENTS.md` children, most READMEs (8 patterns, 66 findings); (5) dead exports kept alive only by their own tests (7 patterns, 71 findings).
- **Three rule/reality conflicts need a decision, not a fix** (§3): `class` (124 in backend, mandated by the backend compass), `noExplicitAny` in tests (biome allows, AGENTS.md forbids), and `globalStyle` in `design-system/charts` (80 calls in one file, vendored Tailwind vocabulary).
- Verification trust: **11 slices H, 2 slices M** (`wallet-b`, `design-system`). Zero fabricated cites found. The only systematic auditor weakness is line-number drift (±1–15 lines, occasionally ±40); verifier-corrected numbers are used throughout.

## 1. Method

**Pipeline.** 13 slices → 13 first-pass auditors (fresh context, read-only tools: `read`/`grep`/`find`/`ls`, no bash) → 13 *independent* verifiers (fresh context, same tools) who re-opened every cite, re-grepped every "no consumer" claim, re-counted every systemic pattern, then opened 5–10 uncited files per slice looking for misses → 13 mechanical merge steps (verifier verdict wins; REJECTED dropped and listed; ADJUSTED severity/tag/scope applied; verifier ADDED findings promoted; IDs renumbered) → this document. The in-session grep census in §4 was produced directly with `rg` at `bdac6c646`.

**Rubric.** Violations of root `AGENTS.md` and the nearest child `AGENTS.md` count as slop; plus the generic taxonomy — COMMENT · DEFENSIVE · ABSTRACTION · NAMING · DUPLICATION · TEST · DOCS · DEAD · TYPE · OVERENG · LOGGING · STYLE. **P1** = structural/systemic (≥5 files or a rule the whole repo claims to enforce), **P2** = local cleanup worth a PR, **P3** = nit. Verifiers demoted every "P1" that named fewer than 5 files; that is why individual P1 rows are rare and the P1 weight sits in the pattern headers.

**Cross-cutting policy applied at merge.** A finding whose only substance is `as any` inside `*.test.ts(x)`, `*.spec.*` or `packages/test-foundation/**` is capped at P3 (biome permits `noExplicitAny` there — `biome.json:101-118`, `:137-146` — while root `AGENTS.md:80` forbids it; see §3).

**Excluded.** `example/**`, `node_modules`, `dist`/`build`/`.build`, `*.gen.ts`, `*.api`, lockfiles, `drizzle/**` migrations/snapshots, `.sst`/`.pulumi`, generated i18n types, minified/vendored files.

**Coverage gaps (honest).** Per-slice "Not reached" lines are in each section. The largest: ~25 chart internals and ~10 `.css.ts` in `design-system`; sharing/pairing view components and most analytics event maps in `wallet-shared`; ~35 leaf `.css.ts` per business slice; 7 of 8 CI workflows and `infra/monitoring/dashboards/*.json`; prestashop `classes/*` beyond five files and wordpress `admin/views`; `apps/shopify/drizzle/**`, `server.js`; `sdk/{android,ios}/README*` and `docs/audits/2026-08-15-*` in the docs slice. Nothing under `example/**`.

**Slice IDs.** WA/WB wallet · BA/BB business · BD/BR backend domain/rest · DS design-system · PS shared packages · SW/SN web/native SDK · LS listener+shopify · PI plugins/infra/scripts · DM docs & meta. Patterns are `XX-Pn`, findings `XX-n`. `Src` column: `A` auditor confirmed · `A*` auditor, adjusted by verifier · `V` verifier-added (109 of 615).

## 2. Cross-cutting systemic themes (rework these first)

Each theme below is the union of per-slice patterns; the per-slice sections carry the cites. File counts are verifier counts.

### 2.1 COMMENT — prose over budget, narrating history or mechanism (18 patterns · 118 findings · every TS slice + both native SDKs)

Root `AGENTS.md` says ≤5 lines, never history, never mechanism, never the other platform. The TS tree has never been gated and it shows: 10–30-line JSDoc essays on hooks, types, `.css.ts` exports and 1-line consts; "used to / previously / the old X / follow-up PR / was considered and rejected" throughout; `§4.8` / `(S1)` / `(audit M-2)` references in `services/backend` to a design document that is not in the repo (BR-P2, ≥55 files repo-wide); ASCII `// =====` banners as file structure (BR-P3, LS-P4, PS-P3 — 160 banner lines in the census); and in the native SDKs, "matching Android / mirrors iOS" comparisons that the linter's phrase list does not catch (SN-P1, 36 files) plus history paraphrased to dodge it (SN-P2).
Members: WA-P1 (≥38 files) · WB-P3 (16) · BA-P3 (≥25) · BD-P1 (22+5) · BR-P1 (≥22) · BR-P2 (≥25/≥55) · BR-P3 (≥16) · DS-P5 (8) · PS-P3 (≥27) · SW-P3 (16) · SW-P5 (12) · SW-P6 (12) · SN-P1 (36) · SN-P2 (11) · LS-P3 (≥20) · LS-P4 (11) · PI-P3 (19) · PI-P4 (16) · BB-P5 (banner half).
**Rework direction.** Extend `scripts/check-comments.ts` to gate `.ts`/`.tsx` (the extension filter at `:68` already accepts them) with a baseline, exactly as was done for native; then trim each block to the single trap. Do BR-P2 first — a citation to a missing document is worse than no comment. Several verifiers flag that many over-budget backend blocks encode real invariants (concurrency, VAT, wire format): the fix is "trim to the trap", not delete.

### 2.2 DUPLICATION — the same body with one literal changed (17 patterns · 77 findings)

Copy-paste families where a `test.each`, a parameter or one shared helper would remove 3–9 files: three `Empty*Modal` components + css + tests (WA-P2, 9 files); demo-mode `await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, N))` inlined 17× with six different N (BB-P2); campaign publish/pause/resume/archive as four 20-line clones plus `mapStatusToActions` declared twice (BB-P3); seven Customize panels on one scaffold (BB-P4); Dialog/AlertDialog/Sheet as three copies of one overlay (DS-P2); relative-date formatter 4×, hash truncation 3×, locale pick 3× in the wallet (WB-P4); four independent wallet-JWT session resolvers in the backend (BR-P4); three copy-pasted "pin-site gate" scripts with `die()` re-declared in 7 files (PI-P1); `services/bootstrap` Postgres URL builder ×4 (PI-P2); each WordPress control schema on five surfaces (PI-P5); identical tiny helpers inside one native platform (SN-P4); listener/shopify handler bodies differing by a method name and a log string (LS-P6).
Members: WA-P2 · WB-P4 · WB-P5 · BB-P2 · BB-P3 · BB-P4 · BB-P6 · BD-P3 · BR-P4 · DS-P2 · DS-P6 · SN-P4 · LS-P6 · PI-P1 · PI-P2 · PI-P5 · DM-P6.

### 2.3 TEST — tests that cannot fail (10 patterns · 64 findings)

`expect(X).toBeDefined()` on an import that resolved; `className.length > 0` on a Vanilla Extract class; `typeof x === "boolean"`; asserting `Intl.DateTimeFormat`; mocking the unit under test and asserting the mock rendered; re-testing TanStack `useMutation` with the action fully mocked; hand-rolled QueryClient harnesses predating the shared `queryWrapper` fixture; ticket IDs baked into native test names (SN-P3). Business `common/` alone has 21 such files (BA-P1); design-system 18 (DS-P3); wallet-shared 11 + 8 (PS-P1/P2).
Members: WA-P4 (13) · BA-P1 (21) · BB-P1 (6) · BD-P4 (7) · DS-P3 (18) · PS-P1 (11) · PS-P2 (8) · SW-P1 (15) · SW-P2 (4) · SN-P3 (6).
**Rework direction.** Delete tautologies outright; convert literal-variant copies to `test.each`; replace mock-only suites with a handful of behavioural tests against real encoders/fixtures. Coverage numbers will drop and that is the point.

### 2.4 DOCS — documentation of a repo that no longer exists (8 patterns · 66 findings)

Every `AGENTS.md` in the Shopify tree and the listener compass names files/symbols that do not exist (LS-P1); the plugin/infra compasses likewise (PI-P7, 7 false claims); "CSS Modules" is still prescribed as the styling system in 15–16 files while zero `.module.css` remain (DM-P1); the vitest project count is stated five different ways, none correct (DM-P2); hand-maintained directory/export inventories have all drifted (DM-P3, 18 files); `CLAUDE.md` duplicates `AGENTS.md` at 4× the length and is wrong wherever they overlap (DM-P4); four parallel agent-instruction trees (`.claude`, `.cursor`, `.opencode`, `AGENTS.md`) are mutually inconsistent (DM-P5, 39 files); SDK and shared-package READMEs are stale against their exports (SW-P7, PS-P4). The Shopify README still says Prisma/SQLite (root `AGENTS.md` itself flags this).
Members: DM-P1 · DM-P2 · DM-P3 · DM-P4 · DM-P5 · LS-P1 · PI-P7 · PS-P4 · SW-P7.
**Rework direction.** Delete inventories (directory trees, export lists, file counts) from every compass — they are what drifts; keep only non-obvious rules. Collapse the four instruction trees to `AGENTS.md` + symlinks. Regenerate or delete `CLAUDE.md`.

### 2.5 DEAD — exports kept alive by their own tests (7 patterns · 71 findings)

Components, hooks, query-key members and fixtures whose only repo-wide reference is their own test file (and sometimes a 2026-07 audit doc): 14 files in wallet-b (WB-P1), 19 in business-a incl. the stranded `cmdk` dependency (BA-P2), 13 exports in listener/shopify (LS-P2), ~250 lines of SDK test scaffolding (SW-P4), test-foundation mocks with no consumer (PS-P5), a Magento webhook-retry layer whose runtime prerequisites do not exist (PI-P6). `knip.ts` exists at the root; these should have been caught — worth checking why they were not (ignore lists, test-file entry points).
Members: WA-P3 · WB-P1 · WB-P2 · BA-P2 · LS-P2 · SW-P4 · PS-P5 · PI-P6 · DM-P5.

### 2.6 Smaller cross-cutting items

- **STYLE / rule violations with a literal ban in `AGENTS.md`:** `globalStyle` in business (BB-P5, 13 files app-wide) and design-system (DS-P4, 7 files; see §3 for charts); `interface` in listener/shopify (LS-P5, 15 declarations) and 70 in `design-system/charts/**` (DS finding, verifier-added); cross-domain imports inside `backend/domain/**` (BD-P2, 4 behavioural edges); four competing className-merge idioms in design-system while `clsx` is a dependency (DS-P1, 15 files).
- **OVERENG:** premature `useMemo`/`useCallback` on trivial expressions (BB-P7; census: 149 wallet, 93 business, 65 design-system); config-driven generics with one participant (WA findings).
- **ABSTRACTION:** zero-value pass-through wrappers around design-system/Radix (BA-P4); 7 true pass-through service methods in backend domain (BD findings).
- **LOGGING:** 32 `console.log` in `services/backend`, 23 in `bootstrap`, 27 in `scripts` (census); per-slice findings where a logger exists and is bypassed.

## 3. Rule vs reality — decisions needed, not fixes

| Rule (root `AGENTS.md:80`) | Reality | Recommendation |
|---|---|---|
| "classes" forbidden | `services/backend` has **124** `class` declarations — every Service/Repository/Orchestrator — and `services/backend/AGENTS.md:52` *mandates* the `{Domain}Context.services.*` singleton shape built on them. 23 more in `apps/wallet` (Tauri/error types), 11 in `wallet-shared`. | The root rule is stale for the backend. Amend it to "no classes in frontend/SDK code; backend domain layer uses classes behind `{Domain}Context`". Not counted as findings in this audit. |
| `as any` forbidden | `biome.json:101-118` turns `noExplicitAny` off for `**/*.test.*`, `**/*.spec.*`; `:137-146` for `packages/test-foundation/**`. Census: 630 `as any` repo-wide, **>96 % in tests**; production hits are 4 in `wallet-shared` (all `biome-ignore`-annotated), 10 in backend, 4 in test-foundation. | Either delete the biome override and pay the debt (listener 130, wallet-shared 201, business 75, wallet 74), or amend `AGENTS.md` to say "in production code". Test-only `as any` findings are capped at P3 in this audit. |
| `globalStyle` forbidden monorepo-wide | 104 calls in `packages/design-system`, **80 of them in `components/charts/charts-utilities.css.ts`** (vendored Tailwind class vocabulary, consumed as string literals); 31 in `apps/business`, 10 in `apps/wallet`. `biome.json:148-152` exempts charts from cognitive complexity only — nothing exempts them from this rule. | Either write the charts exemption down (and stop counting it) or port the chart utilities to sprinkles. The 6 non-chart design-system files and the business/wallet sites are ordinary DS-P4 / BB-P5 findings. |
| Comment budget "enforced" (`bun run lint:comments`) | Gates `.kt`/`.swift` only. `scripts/check-comments.ts:68` accepts `.ts`/`.tsx` but no script passes a TS path. Census of history words alone: backend 66, wallet 46, sdk/core 40, wallet-shared 32 (some are legitimate English — verifiers estimate ⅓–½ are true history narration). | Gate TS with a baseline (same mechanism as `comment-budget-baseline.json`). This single change addresses theme 2.1. |

## 6. Per-slice findings

Each section: coverage · verification line · systemic patterns with cites · findings table (P1 → P2 → P3) · rejected auditor claims · what was checked and found clean.

### Slice: WA — `apps/wallet/app/module/{common,authentication,tokens,walletMerge,recovery-setup}` + `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md`

**Coverage.** ~240 files in slice; ~230 read fully — every `.ts`/`.tsx` source and test plus every `.css.ts` in the five modules, and `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md`.
Skimmed only: `tokens/component/TokenLogo/FrakToken.tsx` (single ~80 KB base64 line, header/tail only).
Nothing in slice unreached; a few cross-slice files (`services/backend/src/api/user/wallet/balance.ts`, `module/recovery/hook/useOnChainRecovery.ts`, `biome.json`) opened only to check usage claims.
Grep-only: the four `module/recovery/hook/*` mutation hooks and the two out-of-slice `formatHash` copies (both later verified).

**Verification.** `confirmed 30 / adjusted 9 / rejected 0 / added 10 · trust H` — every `path:line` opened matched, every "no importer" claim survived an independent repo-wide grep, and the two riskiest claims (`FRK` symbol absent, no ESLint in repo) both hold.
Line drift of 1–5 lines on #5, #10, #20, #26, #27, #31, #33, #35 — corrected cites used below; immaterial.

#### Systemic patterns

##### WA-P1 — COMMENT — P1 — Comment blocks exceed the 5-line budget and narrate mechanism/history (≥30 files auditor, **≥38 verifier**)
AGENTS.md caps comment blocks at 5 lines, forbids history (`used to` / `previously` / `the old X`) and mechanism narration. The slice ignores this wholesale: 10–30-line JSDoc essays on hooks, components, types and even `.css.ts` exports, several longer than the code they sit on, plus explicitly stale facts (no `Navigation` component exists; six modals wrap `DetailOverlay`, not seven). These are design-doc paragraphs that belong in commit messages or `docs/plans/**`; they rot and hide the one trap a reader needs.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/authentication/component/DistantReauthModal/index.tsx:25-54` (30 lines)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/walletMerge/strategy/types.ts:45-71` (27 lines, plus 3 more blocks in the same file)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/common/hook/useWalletSessionGuard.ts:74-82` (7 lines of prose on a 1-line function)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/utils/resolveSelectedToken.ts:8-13` ("The send screen used to hold…")
- `apps/wallet/app/module/common/component/AppShell/index.tsx:42`, `:77` (stale "existing Navigation component"; "removed header area")
- `apps/wallet/app/module/common/component/ModalOutlet/index.tsx:95` ("Seven modals" — verified six)

Rework: trim each block to the single trap (≤5 lines), move rationale to `docs/plans/**`/PR bodies, delete history lines and fix the two stale facts.

##### WA-P2 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Three copy-pasted `Empty*Modal` components (+3 identical css, 3 identical tests) (9 files, verifier 9)
`EmptyPendingGainsModal`, `EmptyTransferModal` and `EmptyTransferredGainsModal` are the same ~60-line component differing only by icon import, i18n prefix and the footer `onClick`. The three `.css.ts` are byte-for-byte identical apart from the exported name, and each has its own one-assert test. Nine files carrying one component's worth of behaviour.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/EmptyPendingGainsModal/index.tsx:16-62`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/EmptyTransferModal/index.tsx:16-63`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/EmptyTransferredGainsModal/index.tsx:16-62`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/EmptyPendingGainsModal/index.css.ts:4-8`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/EmptyTransferModal/index.css.ts:4-8`

Rework: one `EmptyStateModal({ icon, i18nPrefix, action })`; `ModalOutlet` passes the three configs; one css file, one test.

##### WA-P3 — DEAD/STYLE — P2 — Recovery mutation hooks re-export an unused `mutate` alias via `...mutationStuff` (6 in slice, verifier **10 repo-wide**)
Every recovery mutation hook destructures `{ mutate, mutateAsync, ...mutationStuff }` and returns both aliases; the non-async alias has no consumer anywhere in `apps/wallet` — grep returns only definitions, the query-key constant and two `toBeDefined()` assertions. Real but per-site cost is one dead object property, so systemic-P2 rather than P1.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/hook/useSaveRecoveryBlob.ts:13`, `:30-33`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/hook/useDeleteRecoveryBlob.ts:13`, `:30-33`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/hook/useGenerateRecoveryOptions.ts:17`, `:55-58`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/hook/useSendRecoveryTx.ts:30`, `:62-65`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/hook/useConnectedWalletRecovery.ts:10-14` (`...queryStuff`)

Rework: return the `useMutation` result directly, or drop the unused `x: mutate` alias and the `toBeDefined()` tests that pin it.

##### WA-P4 — TEST — P1 — Framework/tautology tests and mock-only assertions (10 files auditor, **13 verifier**)
A repeated test shape exercises TanStack Query, Zustand and module constants rather than behaviour: four copies of "should initialize with correct default state", assertions that a frozen tuple has 2 elements and returns the same reference, variant loops that assert only `getByText`, and duplicate tests that cannot observe what they name (React keys, width/height).
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery-setup/queryKeys/recovery-setup.test.ts:8-32`, `:66-76`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/authentication/hook/useRegister.test.ts:82-93`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/common/component/Skeleton/Skeleton.test.tsx:6-26`, `:56-61`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/authentication/hook/usePreviousAuthenticators.test.tsx:108-118`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/component/TokenListLite/TokenListLite.test.tsx:86-103`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/tokens/utils/validateAmount.test.ts:98-121` (12 cases for a 2-branch function)

Rework: delete framework/constant/loop tests; keep one behaviour test per real branch.

#### Rejected
- None. Every auditor finding reproduced against the source; the nine adjustments are folded into the table above.

#### Checked and clean
- `common/hook/useHardwareBack.test.tsx`, `ErrorBoundary/*`, `ModalErrorToast/*`, `ModalOutlet.test.tsx`, `AppShell.test.tsx`, `DetailOverlay.test.tsx` — behaviour-focused, tight assertions.
- `common/utils/{hostEmbed,sanitizeReturnScheme,sanitizeSeededReward,sanitizeRedirectUrl,shortenAddress}.ts` + tests — small, purposeful, good negative cases.
- `authentication/component/AuthActions.tsx` + test and `authentication/hook/{useRegister,useLogout,useCheckEmail,useCurrentEmail,useLastAuthenticatorHint}.ts` — complex but justified; no structural slop.
- `walletMerge/{stepMachine(.test),utils/*,errors.ts}` — clean logic (comment length only; `errors.ts` doc is WA-16).
- `recovery-setup/utils/{recoveryBlob,recoveryDates}.ts` + tests, and `component/{RecoveryPasswordGate,VerifyPasswordFlow,ValidityDateFields,SummaryRow}` + `useRecoveryAuthorization` — solid round-trip tests, real second callers.
- `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md` — dense, non-obvious, accurate against what was read (SW gate, dual alias, standalone builds).

---

### Slice: WB — `apps/wallet/app/module/{settings,history,monerium,recovery,biometrics,pending-actions,pairing,wallet,...}` + `apps/wallet/app/{entry,routes,...}` + `apps/wallet/src-tauri` (remaining wallet)

**Coverage.** Read fully (~185 files): every `.ts/.tsx` source+test in the 19 in-slice modules, all `app/routes/**`, `app/entry/**`, `app/utils/*.ts`, the vite/vitest/playwright configs, `scripts/**`, `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md`.
Skimmed (headers + test titles): `deepLink.test.ts`, `keyboardInset.test.ts`, `bottomBarRoutes.test.ts`, `VerifyEmail/index.test.tsx`.
Partial: ~15 of ~40 `.css.ts` opened (referral, history, biometrics, pairing, root/register); the rest (explorer, onboarding, wallet, install, entry) not opened.
Not reached: `apps/wallet/{tsconfig.json,package.json,CHANGELOG.md}`, `tests/**` and `tests-light/**` (outside slice).

**Verification.** `confirmed 34 / adjusted 18 / rejected 4 / added 7 · trust M` — every cite the verifier opened resolved to real code and all five systemic patterns held under independent re-grep, but ~⅓ of individual findings needed a severity/tag/premise correction and four were wrong on the facts.
Line-number drift: up to ~15 lines in test files and referral modals (`useGetRewardHistory.test.tsx`, `groupByDay.test.ts`, `ProfilePreferencesCard`, `DeleteRedemptionConfirmModal`); corrected lines used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### WB-P1 — DEAD — P1 — Orphaned exported components/hooks/query keys with no caller repo-wide (7 files auditor, 14 verifier: 8 source + 2 test + 4 orphan `.css.ts`)
Several exported units are referenced only by their own definition, and in two cases only by their own test. A whole-tree grep found no importer. They still cost review, chunk-group regex maintenance in `vite.config.ts`, i18n keys, a 183-line test for an unmounted component, and one carries the wallet's only hardcoded hex colours; `BiometricSettings` is a stale duplicate of the live `BiometricRow`.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/settings/component/LegalLinks/index.tsx:9`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/biometrics/component/BiometricSettings/index.tsx:25` (css hex at `index.css.ts:9-11`; live twin `settings/component/ProfilePreferencesCard/index.tsx:252`)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/notification/component/RemoveAllNotification/index.tsx:21` (+ its test)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/pairing/component/SignatureRequest/index.tsx:25` (`index.css.ts:5` is `style({})`)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/history/queryKeys/history.ts:18,28` (only `historyKey.all` consumed, `walletMerge/hook/useMergeSettle.ts:130`)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/monerium/queryKeys/monerium.ts:16`; `monerium/store/ibanStore.ts:32,101`
Rework: delete the orphaned components (+ their css and tests), the unused query-key members and `clearIbans`.

##### WB-P2 — DEAD — P2 — Dead non-async `mutate` aliases in recovery hooks (9 files auditor, 10 verifier incl. out-of-slice `recovery-setup/`)
Recovery hooks spread `...mutationStuff` and re-export renamed non-async `mutate` aliases (`runRecovery`, `createRecoveryPasskey`, `pushRecoveryPasskey`, `claimRecoveredWallet`) that have exactly one hit each repo-wide: their own `return`. The verifier rejected the other half of the original pattern — returning `error`/`refetch`/`isFetching` from a TanStack-Query wrapper is idiomatic, not slop — hence P2.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery/hook/useRunRecovery.ts:27,63-66`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery/hook/useClaimRecoveredWallet.ts:25,70-73`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery/hook/useCreateRecoveryPasskey.ts:26,50-53`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/recovery/hook/usePushRecoveryPasskey.ts:48,117-120`
Rework: drop the renamed non-async `mutate` aliases; keep the async ones.

##### WB-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — Stale rollout flag + "used to / previously / replaces the legacy" narration (13 files auditor, 16 verifier in-slice)
Comments still gate on `ENSURE_BARE_ARM_ENABLED` / `ROLLOUT-STEP-3`, a flag whose repo-wide hits are now only these wallet comments plus the audit doc that already flagged them; the rest narrate prior implementations, which AGENTS.md explicitly forbids. Test titles carry the same history ("exactly as before", "byte-identical to today").
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/pending-actions/stores/pendingActionsStore.ts:91-92`, `pending-actions/types.ts:26`, `pending-actions/drainEnsures.ts:124-126`, `pendingActionsStore.test.ts:37`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/version/hook/useVersionGate.ts:39-41`; `apps/wallet/app/routes/_wallet/_sso/sso.tsx:192`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/pairing/component/TargetSignatureModal/index.tsx:27`; `explorer/component/ExplorerCard/index.tsx:51`; `settings/component/SecurityProgressCard/index.tsx:14`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/notification/adapter/tauriAdapter.ts:19` + `tauriAdapter.test.ts:5`
- `apps/wallet/vite.config.ts:132-134`; `vite.standalone.config.ts:55-56,61-62`; `install/params.test.ts:48,94,126`; `install/component/InstallView.test.tsx:88,524`
Rework: delete the rollout markers and the replaces/previously/used-to clauses, keep only the invariant each protects, rename history-narrating test titles (`useInstallReferrer.test.ts:127` is the weakest cite — it states a live wire-compat contract).

##### WB-P4 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Relative-date formatter hand-rolled 4×, hash truncation 3×, locale pick 3× (9 files auditor, 10 verifier)
The same today/yesterday/day-month + `toLocaleTimeString` ladder exists four times, differing only in separator and ISO-vs-ms input — and `groupByDay` takes **seconds** while the other three take milliseconds, exactly the divergence this copy-paste produces. Hash truncation exists three more times with different defaults (so different renderings), while `shortenAddress` declares itself the single source of truth.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/history/component/RewardHistory/index.tsx:168-195`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/history/component/MoneriumOrderHistoryItem/index.tsx:177-208`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/history/component/MoneriumOrderDetailModal/index.tsx:237-267` (+ `shortenHash:269-272`)
- `apps/wallet/app/module/history/utils/groupByDay.ts:19-33`
- `apps/wallet/app/module/wallet/component/ExplorerLink/index.tsx:12-22` ↔ `settings/component/ProfileIdentityCard/index.tsx:25-41`
- Locale pick at `settings/component/ProfilePage/index.tsx:44`, `settings/component/ConnectedDevicesPage/DeviceCard.tsx:19`, `common/hook/useDateFormatter.ts:14`
Rework: one `formatRelativeDayTime(ts, locale, t, sep)` in `history/utils`, one hash formatter (extend `shortenAddress`), one locale resolver.

##### WB-P5 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Referral pages: identical Vanilla Extract blocks, modal shells and toast machine copied per component (10 files auditor, 11 verifier)
Card/row/date style blocks are byte-identical across two pages; the 12-line icon-button block is duplicated **four** times (not three); the two confirm modals are the same file save one `whiteSpace`, and the same `ResponsiveModal` + `IconCircle` + two-button TSX; the `"idle" | "shown" | "leaving"` toast machine with `TOAST_EXIT_MS = 220` is copied between a referral page and the register route; `ERROR_KEY_MAP` is duplicated verbatim.
Cites:
- `apps/wallet/app/module/referral/component/ShareReferralCodePage/index.css.ts:5-49` ↔ `RedeemReferralCodePage/index.css.ts:22-59`
- icon button: `ShareReferralCodePage/index.css.ts:27`, `RedeemReferralCodePage/index.css.ts:9`, `ReferralCodeForm/index.css.ts:18`, `onboarding/component/ReferralCodeStep/index.css.ts:15`
- `referral/component/DeleteRedemptionConfirmModal/index.tsx:30-125` ↔ `EditReferralCancelModal/index.tsx:24-96`
- toast: `apps/wallet/app/routes/_wallet/_auth/register.tsx:96-101,236-245` ↔ `ShareReferralCodePage/index.tsx:26,75-84`
- `ReferralCodeForm/index.tsx:33-39` ↔ `AutoGenerateReferralCodeBody/index.tsx:39-45`
Rework: one `ConfirmActionModal` + one css, a shared `referralCard.css.ts`, a `useTransientToast()` hook, hoist `ERROR_KEY_MAP`.

- `auditor #49` — `apps/wallet/app/module/monerium/component/MoneriumBankFlow/*.css.ts` — premise false: per-component sibling sheets are the house style (≥8 counter-examples across the app).
- `auditor #56` — `apps/wallet/scripts/sync-version.sh:1-5`, `tauri-dev.sh:1-2` — headers are the 1-line purpose + usage shape AGENTS.md asks for; the numbered markers are section labels, and `check-comments.ts` does not gate shell.

### Slice: BA — `apps/business/src/module/{common,forms,dashboard,auth,login,settings}` + top-level/config + AGENTS/README

**Coverage.** Read fully (~130 files): app config/entry (`AGENTS.md`, `README.md`, `vite.config.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `tsconfig.json`, `package.json`, `.cta.json`, `doctor.config.json`, `main.tsx`, `router.tsx`) and all logic/test files in `common`, `forms`, `auth`, `dashboard`, `login`, `settings`, plus ~13 `*.css.ts`.
Grep-only: `calendar.css.ts:1-139`; cross-refs into `src/{routes,stores,api}` and `module/{campaigns,members,merchant}`.
Not reached: ~34 leaf `*.css.ts` files with no logic (alert-dialog, command, pagination, billing-*, login-card, …).
Verifier additionally re-grepped repo-wide (all apps) for the dead-code and `globalStyle` claims.

**Verification.** `confirmed 22 / adjusted 8 / rejected 1 / added 8 · trust H` — every cited path/line existed and said substantially what the report claimed, with two factual misclaims and one over-reached pattern. Line-number drift: occasional 1–5 line offsets, never wrong file/section; corrected cites used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### BA-P1 — TEST — P1 — Tests that assert nothing about behaviour (21 files, verifier count)
A large family of tests in `common/component`, `common/utils`, `common/atoms` and `forms` only assert "it rendered", `className.length > 0`, `typeof x === "boolean"`, that a fully mocked dependency rendered its own mock markup, or that `Intl` formats a date. Several names describe behaviour the body never exercises, and some assert the absence of strings that exist nowhere. Zero regression value; they pin implementation details and mock the unit under test. Verifier refinements: `useConversionRate.test.ts:33-152` is copy-paste duplication (real assertions), and `Navigation.test.tsx` also contains genuine behaviour tests, so the pattern is per-test there.
Cites:
- `module/common/component/Command/Command.test.tsx:13-64` — mocks all of `cmdk`, then `:68+` asserts the mock's own testids
- `module/forms/MultiSelect/MultiSelect.test.tsx:71-86` — "calls onValueChange when clear is clicked" never fires an event; `:57-69` asserts `className.length > 0`
- `module/common/utils/openPanel.test.ts:20-33` — `"openPanel" in module`, `typeof === "object" || undefined`; cannot fail
- `module/common/component/Navigation/Navigation.test.tsx:76-81` — asserts absence of strings the nav never renders (tests a deletion)
- `module/common/component/Separator/Separator.test.tsx:6-38` — 4 of 6 tests only `toBeInTheDocument()`
- `module/common/utils/formatDate.test.ts:88-105` — `formatDate(d) === formatDate(d)` self-equality
Rework: delete tautological/mock-only/`Intl` tests, collapse literal-variant copies into `test.each`, keep behaviour tests only.

##### BA-P2 — DEAD — P1 — Orphaned shared components alive only via their own tests (7 components → 19 files, verifier count)
`ActionsWrapper`, `InputAmount`, `TooltipTable`, `Head` (sole importer of `Title`), `MultiSelect` (sole importer of `Command`) have no production importer anywhere in the repo — verifier re-ran the grep across all apps and found only their own tests plus a 2026-07 audit doc. The dead chain also strands the `cmdk` dependency.
Cites:
- `module/common/component/ActionsWrapper/index.tsx:5`, `module/common/component/InputAmount/index.tsx:4`, `module/common/component/TooltipTable/index.tsx:4`
- `module/common/component/Head/index.tsx:13` and `:4` → `Title/index.tsx:28`
- `module/forms/MultiSelect/index.tsx:48` and `:13-21` → `module/common/component/Command/index.tsx`
- `apps/business/package.json:43` (`cmdk`), also referenced at `vite.config.ts:75` and `:64-67`
Rework: delete the seven components with their css/tests, drop `cmdk` from `package.json` and `vite.config.ts`, verify with typecheck.

##### BA-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — Over-budget doc blocks and history narration (≥25 files, verifier count)
Doc blocks routinely exceed the 5-line ceiling and the "never longer than the code" rule, and narrate what the code replaced ("replaces the previous…", "split out of…", "matching the historical default"), both explicitly forbidden by AGENTS.md. Verifier opened 12 files and found the shape uniform across `auth/**` and `settings/**`. Sub-claim adjusted: the dead `§4.x`/`M9`/`B12` design-doc pointers do resolve to nothing in-repo, but the convention is monorepo-wide (`services/backend/src/**`, `apps/shopify/**`), so it drops to P3 and is excluded from this slice's count.
Cites:
- `module/common/hook/useSettingsMerchantId.ts:4-19` — 15 comment lines on a 6-line function, and history narration
- `apps/business/vite.config.ts:20-30` — 10 lines on a 1-line const, pure history; repeats at `:32-35`, `:56-60`, `:88-95`, `:214-220`
- `module/settings/security/SecurityCard/TotpEnrollment.tsx:26-32` — "replaces the previous `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` block"
- `module/settings/billing/validation.ts:1-6` — "split out of `queryKeys.ts`…"
- `module/login/component/Login/breakpoints.ts:1-8` — 9 comment lines for 2 statements
- `module/settings/billing/useBillingAdmin.ts:11-21` — 10-line block on a one-line const
Rework: trim every block to ≤5 lines and shorter than the code; strip "replaces/used to/previously"; move rationale to `docs/plans/**` or the PR body.

##### BA-P4 — ABSTRACTION — P2 — Zero-value pass-through wrappers around design-system/Radix (3 files, verifier count)
`forms/` re-exports DS/Radix components adding nothing but a `ref`/`className`, in one case hand-re-declaring prop unions the DS already owns. Verifier removed `Tooltip` and `Separator` from the pattern (real composition / scoped Vanilla-Extract styling wrappers) and `Command` (counted as dead under BA-P2), dropping the extent below the P1 file bar.
Cites:
- `module/forms/Switch/index.tsx:4-8` — literally `<DSSwitch ref={ref} {...props} />`
- `module/forms/Input/index.tsx:4-16` re-declares `variant`/`length`/`tone`/`error`/`label`/`hint` from `packages/design-system/src/components/Input/index.tsx:7-40`; only added prop is `classNameWrapper ?? className` (`:40`)
- `module/forms/Label/index.tsx:6-12` — Radix `Root` + one class, single consumer (`Form/index.tsx:12`)
Rework: import DS/Radix directly; for `Input` derive `ComponentProps<typeof DSInput>` (DS `InputProps` is not exported) and rename `classNameWrapper` call sites.

### Slice: BB — `apps/business/src/module/{campaigns,merchant,members}` + `apps/business/AGENTS.md`

**Coverage.** ~290 files in slice (campaigns ≈135, merchant ≈115, members ≈55); `apps/business/AGENTS.md` read fully; no module-level AGENTS.md exists.
Read fully (~150 files): all `.ts`/`.tsx` sources and tests in `campaigns/{api,hook,queries,utils,component}`, `merchant/{hook,queries,utils,component}`, `members/{api,queries,component}`, plus `apps/business/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts`.
Skimmed: 8 `.css.ts` files (partial reads).
Not reached: ~35 remaining `.css.ts` (grep'd for `globalStyle` only), 4 webp assets, route files outside the slice (4 opened only to verify dead links).

**Verification.** `confirmed 37 / adjusted 7 / rejected 0 (4 sub-cites dropped) / added 9 · trust H` — every finding the verifier opened matched real code and classifications are mostly sound; only two P1 severities failed the report's own ≥5-file bar.
Line-number drift: auditor cites are routinely off by 1–10 lines and a few ranges overrun the file; verifier-corrected numbers are used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### BB-P1 — TEST — P1 — legacy `merchant/hook/*.test.ts`: hand-rolled QueryClient harness, `as any`, framework/mock tests (6 files, verifier count 6 in slice — 5 with `as any`; habit spans 12 files app-wide)
Six hook tests predate the shared `queryWrapper` fixture, each re-declaring a 15-line `createQueryClient`/`createWrapper` harness, mocking the Eden client with `as any`, and asserting query-key shape, first-render `isLoading`, or `toHaveBeenCalled()` — TanStack Query and the mocks are under test, not the hooks. `usePurchaseWebhookStatus` wraps assertions in `if (…)` so they are vacuous. `useGetMerchantBank.test.ts` has zero `as any` (harness + vacuous asserts only). Note: biome permits `noExplicitAny` in tests (biome.json overrides); AGENTS.md still forbids — policy call; the P1 here rests on the vacuous/framework tests, not the casts alone.
Cites:
- `merchant/hook/useGetMerchantBank.test.ts:33-47` (harness), `:150-162` (key-shape test)
- `merchant/hook/useMerchant.test.ts:9-11` (mocks `merchantQueryOptions`), `:32-46`, `:50-66`
- `merchant/hook/useMerchantSetupStatus.test.ts:78-86` (`as any` ×16 in file)
- `merchant/hook/useGetMerchantAdministrators.test.ts:322-344`, `:346-372`
- `apps/business/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts:265-283` (unused `queryWrapper` target)
Rework: migrate to `queryWrapper`, delete key-shape/loading/`toHaveBeenCalled`-only tests, replace `as any` with typed partial mocks.

##### BB-P2 — DUPLICATION — P1 — demo-mode fake latency + demo branch inlined in every hook (16 files, verifier count 17 in slice / 18 with `common/`)
`await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, N))` with N ∈ {100,200,250,300,500,1000} is pasted into 17 hooks/api files, each usually followed by an identical `queryClient.invalidateQueries(...)` and an `if (isDemoMode) { …sleep…; invalidate; return; }` prologue. Same one-line mechanism, six unexplained constants, no shared helper.
Cites:
- `merchant/hook/useFundTestBank.ts:26`, `merchant/hook/useSetBankOpenStatus.ts:26-31`
- `merchant/hook/useGetMerchantBank.ts:54`, `merchant/hook/useMerchantSetupStatus.ts:88`
- `campaigns/api/mock.ts:143,160`, `campaigns/hook/useSaveCampaign.ts:41`
- `members/api/mock.ts:170` (verifier-added), `Creation/ValidationCampaign/index.tsx:60`
Rework: one `demoDelay()` in `common/atoms/demoMode` plus a `useDemoAwareMutation` wrapper owning the short-circuit + invalidation.

##### BB-P3 — DUPLICATION — P1 — campaign lifecycle transition exists in 5 parallel forms (6 files, verifier count 7)
Publish/pause/resume/archive are four identical 20-line API clones; `mapStatusToActions` is declared verbatim twice; `draftToCampaign` ≈ `buildDemoCampaign`; `useUpdateCampaignRunningStatus` duplicates `useStatusTransition` down to a 4-line comment; `useBulkCampaignActions.runFor` re-wraps the same fns; `CampaignActionModals` holds four ~72-line modal clones differing by icon/i18n key/action literal.
Cites:
- `campaigns/api/campaignApi.ts:203-284` (4 clones), `:15-24` vs `campaigns/api/mock.ts:17-26`
- `campaigns/api/mock.ts:28-46` vs `campaigns/hook/useSaveCampaign.ts:16-31`
- `campaigns/hook/useUpdateCampaignRunningStatus.ts:12-43` vs `useStatusTransition.ts:32-57`
- `campaigns/component/CampaignActionModals/index.tsx:18,91,164,237`
- `campaigns/component/TableCampaigns/useBulkCampaignActions.ts:18-44`
Rework: one `transitionCampaign({merchantId,campaignId,action})` + one config-driven `CampaignActionModal`; delete the duplicate hook/helper.

##### BB-P4 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Customize panels: same form scaffold ×7, same panel body ×2, same preset picker (7 files, verifier count 7)
Every section panel repeats `useMemo(values)` → `useForm({values})` → a `useEffect` that resets the form on `isSuccess` → `useCallback(onSubmit)` → `useCustomizeSection(...)`. `GlobalComponentsPanel` and `PlacementSettingsPanel` are the same component modulo one `FormField` and a `t()` key; `WordingPresets` hand-casts literal strings to `FieldPath` 15 times.

##### BB-P5 — STYLE+COMMENT — P1 — forbidden `globalStyle` + triple-line banner comments (6 css + 5 tsx, verifier count 9 uses / 6 css files in slice; 13 files app-wide)
AGENTS.md forbids `globalStyle` monorepo-wide, yet the slice has 9 uses across 6 `.css.ts` files, three of them byte-identical "hide native spinner" pairs, plus an empty hook-style + descendant selector. The two largest components substitute `/* ------ */` section banners for file splits (`ConfigTab.tsx` ~1255 lines, `RewardCampaign/index.tsx` ~1546 lines).
Cites:
- `campaigns/component/Creation/BudgetCampaign/budget.css.ts:41,48`; `RewardCampaign/reward.css.ts:30,34`; `ReferralChainCampaign/referralChain.css.ts:20,24`

- `campaigns/component/CampaignDetailsSheet/ConfigTab.tsx:123-125,441-443,1063-1065`
- `campaigns/component/Creation/RewardCampaign/index.tsx:100-102`
Rework: shared `numberInputNoSpinner` class + scoped classes instead of `globalStyle`; split the two oversized components into per-section files.

##### BB-P6 — DUPLICATION — P2 — bank on-chain mutation hooks share one 40-line body (4 files, verifier count 4 — below the ≥5 P1 bar)
Four hooks repeat: demo branch → `sendTx({tx:{to,data}, metadata:{header,i18n:{fr,en}}})` → `waitForTxAndInvalidateQueries({hash, queryKey: merchantBankQueryKey})`. `useBankAllowanceMutation` additionally holds two 30-line branches differing only by `functionName`/`args`/copy. `useMigrateLegacyBank` legitimately diverges (3-tx batch, second key), so extraction is less clean than described.
Cites:
- `merchant/hook/useBankAllowanceMutation.ts:43-114`
- `merchant/hook/useSetBankOpenStatus.ts:24-63`
- `merchant/hook/useWithdrawFromBank.ts:40-72`
- `merchant/hook/useMigrateLegacyBank.ts:34-99`
Rework: `useBankTx({merchantId, bankAddress})` returning `(data: Hex, copy) => Promise<void>`; callers only `encodeFunctionData`.

##### BB-P7 — OVERENG — P2 — premature `useMemo`/`useCallback` on trivial expressions (8 files cited, verifier count ~5 clearly slop)
Memoisation of `.find()`, `isAddress()`, `?? {}`, `.some()` and 2-line callbacks with no identity-dependent consumer. Verifier excludes `TableCampaigns/index.tsx:48-54` (prop object identity) and `KpiCardsRow.tsx:48-70` (four `Intl.NumberFormat` constructions) as legitimate.
Cites:
- `campaigns/component/TableCampaigns/Filter.tsx:44-63`
- `campaigns/component/TableCampaigns/index.tsx:29-32,42-45`
- `merchant/component/ButtonAddTeam/index.tsx:52-58`
- `members/component/TableMembers/Pagination.tsx:9-16`
- `campaigns/component/Overview/KpiCardsRow.tsx:85-91`
Rework: inline; keep `useMemo` only where a downstream effect or table `columns` depends on identity.

### Slice: BD — `services/backend/src/domain/**` + `services/backend/AGENTS.md`

**Coverage.** Read fully (~200 of 206 `.ts` files): every `context.ts`, `index.ts`, `db/schema.ts`, `schemas/`, `dto/`, `models/`, all repositories/services in all 16 domains, plus ~33 test files and `services/backend/AGENTS.md`.
Read partially: `campaign/services/RewardCalculator.test.ts` (1–350/829), `merchant/services/MerchantRegistrationService.test.ts` (1–120/420), `merchant/services/OwnershipTransferService.test.ts` (1–100/350); skimmed `campaign-bank/abis.ts`.
Not reached: `billing/services/BillingComputationService.test.ts` (verifier opened it — clean). No `AGENTS.md` exists under `src/domain`.
Grep-only (cross-slice, for caller counts): `src/api`, `src/orchestration`, `src/jobs`, `src/utils`, `src/infrastructure`.

**Verification.** `confirmed 34 / adjusted 8 / rejected 2 / added 9 · trust H` — every claim the verifier opened existed and was classified sensibly; only two findings collapsed under type/caller checks.
Line-number drift is systematic but small: typically ±5, occasionally ±40 (auditor #27 cited `243-249` for code at `353-358`; PATTERN-5's `buildTimeContext` cite is `667-688`, not `628-647`). Corrected lines are used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### BD-P1 — COMMENT — P1 — doc blocks far over the 5-line budget, many narrating history or mechanism (22+ source files + 5 test files, verifier count)
JSDoc/inline blocks of 8–26 lines sit on repositories, schemas and services; several are pure history ("used to", "previously", "follow-up PR"), one narrates a rejected alternative, several restate the SQL beneath, and two are written twice in the same method. AGENTS.md caps blocks at 5 lines and forbids history/mechanism narration; the TS tree is the one place `scripts/check-comments.ts` does not gate, and it shows. A large share of these blocks do encode real concurrency/VAT/wire-format invariants — the fix is "trim to the trap", not "delete".
Cites:
- `identity/repositories/WalletBindingRepository.ts:159-171`, `:239-262`, `:328-333` (cache-eviction rationale written a second time in the same method)
- `business-auth/repositories/BusinessAccountRepository.ts:9-15` (pure history: "Absorbs what used to be three repositories")
- `business-auth/db/schema.ts:31-49` ("A jsonb `credentials` blob was considered and rejected")
- `auth/db/schema.ts:4-21` ("They get dropped in a follow-up PR once every env has migrated")
- `campaign/services/CampaignManagementService.ts:239-245` (two consecutive comments on `withStartGate`)
- `identity/repositories/InstallCodeRepository.test.ts:15-18`, `:150-158` (explicit "is NOT covered here" notes)
Rework: trim every block to ≤5 lines keeping only the trap/invariant, move history and rejected alternatives to `docs/plans/**`, and extend `scripts/check-comments.ts` gating to `services/backend/src`.

##### BD-P2 — STYLE — P1 (doc) / P2 (code) — cross-domain imports inside `domain/**` bypass the orchestration rule (10 non-test files, verifier count)
Services, contexts, types and schemas reach into sibling domains directly. `services/backend/AGENTS.md:38-42` and root AGENTS.md say cross-domain logic lives only in `src/orchestration/`. Verifier narrowing: of 11 hits in 10 files, 6 are type/schema-only imports (coupling, no runtime call path, not forbidden by either doc); only 4 are behavioural edges. The P1 is the doc/code disagreement; the code move is P2-sized.
Cites:
- `campaign-bank/services/CampaignBankService.ts:5` (service → foreign `MerchantRepository`; also *writes* `merchant.bankAddress` at `:32-36`)
- `campaign-bank/context.ts:1` (context → `MerchantContext`)
- `campaign/services/RuleEngineService.ts:3` (service → `rewards` `AssetLogRepository`)
- `campaign/context.ts:2` (context → `RewardsContext`)
- `rewards/repositories/AssetLogRepository.ts:20-21` (joins `identity/db/schema` + `merchant/db/schema` — schema-only)
Rework: move the 4 behavioural edges into `src/orchestration/`, and amend the AGENTS.md rule to state explicitly whether type/schema-only imports are allowed.

##### BD-P3 — DUPLICATION — P1 — the same type/constant/rule declared in two or three places (14 files, verifier count)
Types, rate constants and state-machine rules exist in multiple hand-maintained copies, and one pair has already drifted representation (string vs `Decimal`). Verifier found it understated: the whole `campaignBankAbi` literal is a verbatim copy of an app-essentials export, and `merchant/schemas/index.ts` mirrors five component schemas field-by-field.
Cites:
- `campaign/types/index.ts:20-25` (hand-written `BudgetUsed`) vs `campaign/schemas/index.ts:204-210` (`Static<>`), both in live use
- `attribution/services/ReferralService.ts:7-10` vs `attribution/repositories/ReferralLinkRepository.ts:11-14` vs `campaign/types/index.ts:151-154` (`ReferralChainMember` ×3)
- `billing/services/BillingComputationService.ts:21-22` (`"0.20"`) vs `billing/services/pdf/primitives.ts:27-28` (`new Decimal("0.20")`)
- `campaign/services/CampaignManagementService.ts:310-315` vs `campaign/repositories/CampaignRuleRepository.ts:270-290` (status transitions encoded twice)
- `campaign-bank/repositories/CampaignBankRepository.ts:227-285` / `:287-352` (`grant`/`revokeManagerRole` differ only by `functionName`)
Rework: one declaration per type (derive from the TypeBox schema), one transition table (repository side), one VAT/fee constant module consumed by computation and PDF, and `setManagerRole(kind)`.

##### BD-P4 — TEST — P1 — tests that assert only on their own mocks, smoke-assert, or test the runtime (7 files, verifier count)
Suites configure a mock and then assert the mock's value, duplicate an earlier case verbatim, or assert only that bytes were produced — so the behaviour named in the test title is never exercised. One fixture even passes fields the DTO forbids, invisible because `tsconfig.json:2` excludes `**/*.test.ts` from typecheck.
Cites:
- `attribution/repositories/ReferralLinkRepository.test.ts:117-155` (`mockExecute` returns `would_cycle:true`, asserts `true`; the CTE is never run)
- `notifications/services/NotificationsService.test.ts:199-212` ("handles sending errors gracefully" configures no error), `:309-326` (same assertion as `:177-197`)
- `auth/services/WalletSdkSessionService.test.ts:137-146` (asserts a rejected mock rejects), `:55-59` (fields the DTO at `auth/models/WalletSessionDto.ts:54-61` disallows)
- `billing/services/pdf/index.test.ts:96-158` (9 renders asserting `length > 0` / `%PDF-`)
- `campaign/services/RuleEngineService.test.ts:20-22` (empty `beforeAll`), `:667-688` (`toHaveProperty` enumeration)
Rework: delete the mock-echo and tautological cases, keep the cache/idempotency cases, and for PDF assert on extracted text or keep one smoke test per kind.

#### Rejected
- `auditor #21` — `campaign-bank/repositories/CampaignBankRepository.ts:389-417` — the `contracts` array is heterogeneous (`isOpen`→boolean, `getBalance`/`getAllowance`→bigint), so viem types each result `boolean | bigint`; the `typeof` checks are required narrowing and the proposed rework would not type-check.
- `auditor #41` — `rewards/utils.ts` — the helper has two consumers in different layers (`rewards/repositories/InteractionLogRepository.ts:137`, `orchestration/PurchaseInteractionCreator.ts:70`) and centralises a persisted key format; inlining would force the orchestrator to import repository internals.

#### Checked and clean
- Layering basics hold: no `service → orchestrator` import in `domain/**`, no `new Service()` outside `context.ts`, no `console.*` / `@ts-ignore` / `as any` in non-test source, no `.skip`/`.todo`.
- `identity/repositories/{IdentityRepository,InstallCodeRepository,EmailVerificationRepository,RecoveryRepository}.ts` — comments are genuine traps; SQL races handled in-DB.
- `business-auth/services/*` (Password, Totp, EmailOtp, BusinessSession, ShopifySso, BusinessAccount) and tests — behaviour-level with real crypto round-trips; `attemptGuard.ts` is a justified pure helper.
- `campaign/services/RuleConditionEvaluator.ts` + golden-corpus test, `CampaignManagementService.test.ts` validation suites, `RewardCalculator.ts` FX handling, `campaign/schemas/merchantRewardParity.ts` (deliberate compile-time parity check).
- `purchases/dto/lineItem.ts` + test, `PurchaseRepository.ts` (SQL-rendered assertions), `PurchaseClaimRepository.ts`; `rewards/services/SettlementService.ts` + reconciliation-matrix test, `InteractionLogRepository.ts`.
- `billing/services/BillingComputationService.ts` (+ its test, re-verified independently), `pdf/primitives.ts` `sanitizeForWinAnsi`, `notifications/services/FcmSender.ts` HTTP/2 lifecycle, `pairing/repositories/*`, `affiliate/**`, `media/**`, `identity/services/IdentityProofService.ts`, `campaign-bank/computeDistributionStatus.ts`.

### Slice: BR — `services/backend/src/{api,orchestration,infrastructure,utils,jobs}`, top-level, scripts, tests

**Coverage.** ~260 files in slice; ~150 read fully: all of `src/utils/**`, `src/jobs/**`, `src/infrastructure/**`, all `src/api/**` routes/middleware/schemas, `src/{index,debug,legacyRoutes,global.d}.ts`, `build.ts`, `vitest.config.ts`, `AGENTS.md`, `src/api/README.md`, 4 `scripts/*.ts`, and most of `src/orchestration/**` (context, all top-level orchestrators + tests, reward, interaction-submission, campaigns, identity, pairing, referral-code, billing, schemas).
Skimmed (first 60–130 lines): 4 identity/billing test files, `campaignOverviewSchemas.ts`, `DnsCheckRepository.test.ts`.
Not reached: `scripts/walletHistory.ts` (grep only), 3 `orchestration/schemas/*Schemas.ts`, 4 orchestrator test files, `utils/{userAgent,shopDomainMatch}.test.ts`, `CHANGELOG.md`, `docs/product-scoped-campaigns.md`, `services/backend/test/**` (outside slice).
**Verification.** `confirmed 41 / adjusted 9 / rejected 1 / added 8 · trust H` — every `path:line` the verifier opened matched the claimed content and the two highest-value claims (the `referral_arrival` dead literal, the missing design doc behind the `§x.y` references) held under independent grep; the only systematic weakness was P1 assigned to 2-file duplications.
Line drift: only 2 cites drifted by a handful of lines, none wrong; corrected line numbers are used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### BR-P1 — COMMENT — P1 — comment blocks over the 5-line ceiling / longer than the code they sit on (≥20 files auditor, ≥22 in-slice verifier)
JSDoc and `//` blocks of 10–25 lines sitting above 1–5-line declarations across infra, utils, orchestration and schemas — rationale paragraphs that AGENTS.md says belong in the commit, PR or `docs/plans/**`. Verifier opened 13 cited sites and 12 held (`IdentityMergeService.ts:30-72` dropped as a weak cite: groups are 2–4 lines against a 40-line type), and added 7 uncited files. Caveat: `scripts/check-comments.ts` gates `.kt`/`.swift` only, so TS is un-gated — but the root anti-pattern list is monorepo-wide.
Cites:
- `src/infrastructure/macro/authError.ts:1-23` — 22 lines of prose on a 1-line `const`
- `src/utils/elysiaNoContentPatch.ts:3-19` — 17 lines on a 6-line plugin
- `src/api/common/version.ts:4-18` (15 lines on a 4-line const) and `:30-37`
- `src/orchestration/schemas/walletMergeSchemas.ts:50-58,60-68,74-86` — 7–12-line docs on 1-line fields
- `src/orchestration/TakeAdsIngestionOrchestrator.ts:50-70` — four constants each with 3–5-line rationale
- `src/infrastructure/keys/AdminWalletsRepository.ts:90-103` — 12 lines on a 4-line method

Rework: cut each block to ≤5 lines naming the trap; prioritise the rationale/history prose (BR-P2) over trap comments running 2 lines over.

##### BR-P2 — COMMENT — P1 — history narration and `§x.y` / audit-id references to a document that does not exist (≥30 files auditor, ≥25 in-slice / ≥55 repo-wide verifier)
Comments recording what the code used to do, plus dozens of `§4.8`, `(S1)`, `(B4)`, `(R3)`, `(audit M-2)` references to a design doc and audit list absent from the repo — `services/backend/docs/` holds only `product-scoped-campaigns.md` and `docs/plans/**` is native-SDK/frontend. The convention has leaked out of the backend (`apps/business/src/module/common/hook/useLogout.ts:11`). Verifier opened 9 of 13 history cites, all exact; a raw grep overstates it (~1/3 of ~30 hits are genuine history prose, the rest identifiers).
Cites:
- `src/utils/stepUpRequired.ts:24-26` and `src/utils/siwe.ts:48-50` — "three call sites previously reimplemented"
- `src/orchestration/identity/WalletSessionOrchestrator.ts:19-20`; `src/orchestration/campaigns/queries.ts:11-13`

##### BR-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — narration and section-banner comments in legacy infra & api files (≥15 files auditor, ≥16 in-slice verifier)
`// Get the X`, `// Return the Y`, `// ===`/`// ---` banners, and JSDoc restating the signature — including a doc block copy-pasted from `getKeySpecificAccount` onto `getMutexForAccount` and the same "Slack always returns HTTP 200" note three times in one file. - `src/infrastructure/pricing/PricingRepository.ts:53-71` ("// Cache for the prices", "// The ky client", "// Build our ky client")

##### BR-P4 — DUPLICATION — P2 — four independent wallet-JWT session resolvers (4 files, verifier confirms 4)
The `x-wallet-auth` → `JwtContext.wallet.verify` → 401-with-`AUTH_ERROR_HEADER` block is re-implemented four times; `walletGroupContext` re-verifies the JWT even though it `.use(sessionContext)`, and `ws.ts` adds a redundant cast of an already-typed result. Downgraded from P1: 4 files is below the report's own ≥5-file bar, `ws.ts` reads the JWT off a WebSocket query string rather than the header (cousin, not copy), and Elysia macros cannot consume another macro's `resolve`, so this is a hoist rather than a delete.
Cites:
- `src/infrastructure/macro/session.ts:73-90` and `:121-137`
- `src/api/middleware/identity.ts:36-45`
- `src/api/user/wallet/auth/walletGroupContext.ts:26-38` (uses `sessionContext` at `:22`)
- `src/api/user/wallet/pairing/ws.ts:23-31` (redundant cast at `:26-29`; `infrastructure/external/jwt.ts:227`)

Rework: hoist the 401-tagging into one shared helper and compose `walletGroupContext`/`identityContext` on `sessionContext`'s resolved `walletSession`.

### Slice: DS — `packages/design-system/**` + `packages/ui-preview/**` + `packages/AGENTS.md`

**Coverage.** Read fully (~142 files): all 58 `components/<Name>/index.tsx` + 52 co-located tests, ~35 `.css.ts`, tokens/theme/sprinkles/reset/keyframes/breakpoints, `hooks/*`, `hostSheet.ts`, 34 icon files, both AGENTS.md, and all of `packages/ui-preview` bar `explorer-phone.css.ts`.
Skimmed (partial): `charts/{chart-context,bar-chart,bar,time-series-chart-shell,area-chart,use-chart-interaction}`, `charts/tooltip/chart-tooltip`; ~60 remaining icons via grep (uniform template).
Not reached: ~25 chart internals (`pie-*`, `grid`, `numeric-y-axis`, `reference-line`, `decimate-time-series`, `chart-defs`, …), `charts/tooltip/{date-ticker,tooltip-content,tooltip-dot,tooltip-indicator}`, ~10 `.css.ts` (Checkbox, Switch, TimeInput, ToastSurface, ProgressBar, …), `ui-preview/src/explorer-phone/explorer-phone.css.ts`.

**Verification.** `confirmed 32 / adjusted 5 / rejected 0 / added 9 · trust M` — every cited file, symbol and code shape the verifier opened matched the claim, with no fabricated evidence, but the report missed the slice's largest rule violation (70 `interface` declarations under `charts/**`).
Line-number drift is systematic: ±1–2 on most JSDoc cites, up to 8 on `DataTable/index.tsx` (`:341-346` → `:349-354`) and 5 on `:120-122` → `:115-117`; corrected numbers are used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### DS-P1 — STYLE — P1 — Four competing className-merge idioms in one package (15 files off-idiom, verifier count 15)
`clsx` is a direct dependency (`design-system/package.json:49`) and is imported in 32 component files, yet three hand-rolled idioms coexist for the identical job: template literals, `[a,b].filter(Boolean).join(" ")`, and `className ? \`${base} ${className}\` : base`. Each is a re-implementation a reader must re-verify; `InAppBanner` alone carries six hand-rolled merges in one JSX tree.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/InAppBanner/index.tsx:35,40,45,52,58,67`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/index.tsx:29,48,76`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/AlertDialog/index.tsx:30,49,84`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Tabs/index.tsx:34,56,73`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Button/index.tsx:79`, `components/Card/index.tsx:25,44`
Rework: one mechanical sweep replacing every hand-rolled merge with `clsx(base, className)`.

##### DS-P2 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Dialog / AlertDialog / Sheet are three copies of one overlay (6 source + 2 tests, verifier count 6+2)
`dialog.css.ts` and `alertDialog.css.ts` are identical for keyframes, content, title and description (only `zIndex.modal + 1` vs `+ 3`; AlertDialog additionally adds an overlay specificity hack, so "byte-for-byte" overstates it). `sheet.css.ts` re-declares the title/description a third time, and the two component bodies are the same modulo the Radix namespace — a padding tweak lands in three places.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/dialog.css.ts:10-30` ≡ `components/AlertDialog/alertDialog.css.ts:23-44`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/dialog.css.ts:32-44` ≡ `components/AlertDialog/alertDialog.css.ts:46-58` ≡ `components/Sheet/sheet.css.ts:149-160`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/index.tsx:23-36` ≡ `components/AlertDialog/index.tsx:23-37`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/Dialog.test.tsx:14-24` ≈ `components/AlertDialog/AlertDialog.test.tsx:14-28`
Rework: one `modalSurface.css.ts` parameterised by z-index via `style([shared, {zIndex}])`; Dialog/AlertDialog import the shared classes.

##### DS-P3 — TEST — P1 — Tautological "is defined" / "className is truthy" tests (18 files, verifier count 18)
Seven test files enumerate `expect(X).toBeDefined()` over every compound export; eleven more render N variants and assert only that a `<span>` mounted or that a Vanilla Extract class is non-empty. An import that resolved cannot be undefined and a VE class is never empty, so these cases cover no behaviour; two assert the wrong element outright.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/Dialog.test.tsx:56-61`, `components/Sheet/Sheet.test.tsx:67-72`, `components/Select/Select.test.tsx:141-148`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Badge/Badge.test.tsx:17-31` (and `:33-37`, same tautology)
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Box/Box.test.tsx:15-23`, `components/Columns/Columns.test.tsx:23-35,68-79`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Tooltip/Tooltip.test.tsx:36-45` (asserts the trigger, not the content)
Rework: delete the `toBeDefined` cases; assert recipe classes as `Sheet.test.tsx:76-127` and `IconCircle.test.tsx` already do.

##### DS-P4 — STYLE — P1 — `globalStyle` descendant selectors despite the monorepo-wide ban (7 files, verifier count 7)
Root `AGENTS.md` forbids `globalStyle` and names the `${parent} *` shape exactly; six component `.css.ts` files use it, and `charts/charts-utilities.css.ts` adds 127 calls that reimplement Tailwind's class vocabulary (`.w-full`, `.gap-1\.5`, `.text-xs`) consumed as string literals — while Tailwind itself is a root anti-pattern. The vendoring note is honest, but the exemption is written nowhere; `biome.json:148-152` exempts charts from cognitive complexity only.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/charts-utilities.css.ts:14-140`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/chartsTheme.css.ts:9` (verifier-added cite)
- `packages/design-system/src/components/DataTable/data-table.css.ts:68,73,83,87,91,96,100,104`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Spread/index.css.ts:10`, `components/Inline/index.css.ts:7`, `components/Tiles/index.css.ts:22`
Rework: put child classes on mapped children / reuse `Table` recipe classes; for charts either convert to `style()` or write the exemption into `design-system/AGENTS.md`.

##### DS-P5 — COMMENT — P1 — JSDoc restating the export name on Radix pass-throughs (8 files, verifier count 8)
Every compound-component file carries a 3-line block on each re-export saying what the identifier already says (`/** Stateless root — pairs trigger + content. */` above `export const Dialog = RadixDialog.Root;` and ~20 siblings) — roughly 60 lines of zero-information prose against the comment budget.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Dialog/index.tsx:10-12,15-17,55-57`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Sheet/index.tsx:19-21,24-26,29-31,34-36`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/AlertDialog/index.tsx:11-13,16-18`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/Drawer/index.tsx:31-33,115-117,126-128,143-145,150-152`
Rework: delete them; keep only blocks naming a real trap, trimmed to ≤5 lines.

##### DS-P6 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Chart axis/tooltip repeat one label body and one mount gate (4 files, verifier count 4)
`XAxisLabel` and `BarXAxisLabel` are the same ~40-line function — identical fade ramp and zero-width-container layout, differing only in `<span>` vs `<motion.span>`. The `useState(false)` + `useEffect(setMounted)` + `containerRef.current` portal gate is copy-pasted into four components, so one fix lands in 2–4 places.
Cites:
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/x-axis.tsx:27-71` ≡ `charts/bar-x-axis.tsx:30-70`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/x-axis.tsx:73-86`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/bar-x-axis.tsx:73-89`
- `packages/design-system/src/components/charts/tooltip/tooltip-box.tsx:39-53`
Rework: one `AxisLabel` with an `animate` flag plus one `usePortalContainer(containerRef)` hook (or drop the SSR-only gate).

#### Rejected

None.

#### Checked and clean

- Layout primitives `Box`, `Stack`, `Inline`, `Columns`, `Column` (logic), `Tiles`, `Spread`, `ContentBlock`, `HiddenVisually` — small, typed, no defensive code.
- `Input`, `TextArea`, `FieldLabel`, `useFieldIds`, `fieldChrome.css.ts` — cohesive; labelled-mode tests (`Input.test.tsx:97-260`, `TextArea.test.tsx:56-230`, `FieldLabel.test.tsx`) are genuine a11y/DOM behaviour tests.
- `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `RadioGroup`, `Tabs`, `Accordion`, `Stepper`, `ProgressBar`, `DeltaIndicator`, `Notice`, `AlertMessage`, `FieldError`, `LegendItem`, `IconCircle`, `Table`, `ToastSurface`, `ConfirmationTooltip`, `GlassButton`, `TimeInput`, `FunnelChart` — behaviour-focused, short trap comments.
- `Sheet.test.tsx:76-127`, `IconCircle.test.tsx`, `Spinner.test.tsx` — the correct model for variant assertions; `PullToRefresh` ref-based logic is justified (only its comments are flagged).
- `tokens.css.ts`, `theme.css.ts`, `sprinkles.css.ts` (bar the dark-mode condition), `reset*.css.ts`, `defaults.css.ts`, `keyframes.css.ts`, `hostSheet.ts` + test, `useResponsiveValue` + test, `global.ts`, package exports maps, both `vitest.config.ts`.
- `ui-preview/src/social/*`, `explorer-phone/index.tsx`, `preview-frame/*`, `sdk-components/index.tsx` bodies, `utils/variables.test.tsx` — live and consumed by business/shopify.
- Icons other than those flagged: uniform 20-line template, `currentColor`, `aria-hidden`, `{...props}` last.

### Slice: PS — `packages/{wallet-shared,app-essentials,rpc,test-foundation,dev-tooling,client}`

**Coverage.** Read fully (~115 files): all of `packages/client`, `dev-tooling`, `rpc` (minus ABI dumps/configs), `test-foundation` `src/*`, `app-essentials` core sources + tests, and the bulk of `wallet-shared` (stores, authentication, blockchain, smartWallet, pairing, common, analytics, sharing, referral, i18n, utils, providers) plus all package `AGENTS.md`/README/`knip.ts`.
Skimmed/partial: large mock-heavy test suites (`useLogin.test.tsx`, `connector.test.ts`, `provider.test.ts`, `FrakEcdsa*`, `origin.test.ts`, `safeSession.test.ts`, CHANGELOGs, `kernelAccountAbis.ts`).
Not reached: sharing sub-components + `.css.ts` and their tests, pairing view components/tests, most analytics event maps, `ssoDataCompression`, `identity/*`, many hook/util tests, locale JSONs, tsconfigs, ABI dumps.
`as any` census: ~205 hits; only four production hits, all `biome-ignore`-annotated (`wallet/smartWallet/connector.ts:72,86`, `polyfills/bigint-serialization.ts:3,5`); the rest are tests plus an un-annotated `test-foundation` cluster.

**Verification.** `confirmed 38 / adjusted 6 / rejected 0 / added 6 · trust H` — every cite the verifier opened matched file, line and content, all five patterns survived an independent re-count, and the report reads conservative rather than inflated.
Line-number drift: only one slip (#14, `rpc/src/client.ts:243-250` → `:370-375`); several cites tightened by 1–3 lines (e.g. `crashlytics.ts:64-66`→`:62-64`, `useGetUserBalance.test.tsx:157-181`→`:161-187`), corrected below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### PS-P1 — TEST — P1 — Tautological tests of constants / library shapes (10 files auditor, 11 verifier)
Whole test files enumerate properties of constant objects or third-party values — "is an array with 2 elements", "same reference twice", `typeof x === "string"`, `toHaveProperty("id")` on viem's own chain object, or re-implementing the code under test and comparing it to itself. They cannot fail unless TypeScript already failed, so they add suite time and inflate coverage without covering behaviour.
Cites:
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/authentication/queryKeys/auth.test.ts:10,18` (22 `it`s over a 5-key const; `:105-118` repeats it)
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/i18n/config.test.ts:6-8,30-32,43-45`; `i18n/index.test.ts:4-12` (asserts a barrel re-exports)
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/blockchain/provider.test.ts:11-16,39-60`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/common/lib/inApp.test.ts:33-42` (asserts jsdom, not the module)
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/wallet/action/registerOptions.test.ts:6-11,33-40`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/pairing/queryKeys/index.test.ts:32,53,57,92,170`
Rework: delete the shape tests; keep one `toEqual` per key factory where the literal matters, and one frozen-date assertion for `registerOptions`.

##### PS-P2 — TEST — P1 — Mock-everything suites that assert only on mocks (8 files, verifier 8)
Every dependency is `vi.mock`ed, then the test asserts `toHaveBeenCalledWith` on the mock, `toBeDefined()` on a mocked return, or `toBeInstanceOf(Function)`; each test re-`await import`s already-hoisted modules and casts inputs `as any`. The tests re-state the implementation's call graph, so any refactor breaks them and a real wiring bug in `baseFrakWallet`/`createConfig` stays invisible.
Cites:
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/wallet/smartWallet/FrakSmartWallet.test.ts:6-27,32-38,61-66,103-104` (asserts the mocked `toHex`/`keccak256` were called)
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/blockchain/aa-provider.test.ts:3-18,31,36-40`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/wallet/smartWallet/provider.test.ts:6-56,59-80`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/providers/BaseProvider.test.tsx:41-65`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/wallet/smartWallet/webAuthN.test.ts:27-30`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/wallet/smartWallet/connector.test.ts:22,38-43,63-65`
Rework: replace with behavioural tests against real viem encoders (golden `initCode`/signature hex); do not extend to `signature.test.ts`, which asserts real values.

##### PS-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — Banners, history narration and over-budget comment blocks (≥25 files auditor, ≥27 verifier)
`// ====` section banners, "used to / no longer / originally / previously" narration, and JSDoc longer than the code it sits on — the exact shapes root AGENTS.md forbids, unenforced here because `check-comments.ts` does not gate TS. The history prose belongs in commit messages or `docs/plans/**`, and the oversized blocks mostly restate mechanism.
Cites:
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/authentication/webauthn/tauriBridge.ts:26,28,63,65,105-128` (14 banner lines; 24-line block)
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/pairing/clients/base.ts:189,191,268,270,310,312,398,400`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/stubs/tauri-noop.ts:68` ("Historic … no longer imports"); `sharing/buildSharingLink.ts:12`; `i18n/locales/en/index.ts:5`
- `packages/wallet-shared/src/common/lib/ua.ts:1-21` (21 lines over a 15-line module)
- `packages/test-foundation/src/dom-mocks.ts:22-34,44-55,64-76,85-108`; `vitest.shared.ts:1-27,56`
- `packages/rpc/src/types.ts:180-235`; `packages/wallet-shared/src/identity/mergeTokenQueryOptions.ts:25-46` (verifier-added)
Rework: delete banners, move history to commits/plans, cap blocks at 5 lines and never longer than the body — `dev-tooling` prose is genuine trap knowledge and the lowest-priority target.

##### PS-P4 — DOCS — P1 — README/AGENTS prose stale against the code it describes (7 docs auditor, 8 verifier)
Package docs describe APIs, files, mocks and exports that do not exist, wrapped in marketing tone and emoji checklists. The rpc fiction also ships in the published types, so consumers read a `connect()`/`stream()` API the client never had.
Cites:
- `packages/rpc/README.md:44-48,53,64,133-142,211,3,7` vs real `request`/`listen`/`sendLifecycle`/`cleanup` at `packages/rpc/src/client.ts:33-38,130-136`; `packages/rpc/EXAMPLE.md:15,20,56,99,125`
- `packages/test-foundation/README.md:3,96-110,205,540-541` vs `src/vitest.shared.ts:152-159` and `wallet-shared/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts:313-338`
- `packages/test-foundation/AGENTS.md:22,26` vs `src/wallet-mocks.ts:22`, `src/vitest.workers.ts:11-13`; `src/apps-setup.ts:12-14`
- `packages/wallet-shared/AGENTS.md:6,10,20` (`walletStore`, `providers/FrakContext.tsx`, `AlertDialog` — none exist)
- `packages/app-essentials/AGENTS.md:11,13,17,19` vs `src/blockchain/platform.ts:47,56,67`
Rework: rewrite the rpc README to the real 4-method API and drop EXAMPLE.md; cut the test-foundation README to setup order + real fixtures; regenerate the three AGENTS "Key Files/Exports" sections from the barrels.

##### PS-P5 — DEAD — P2 — Exported test scaffolding and mocks with no consumer (4 files auditor, 5 verifier)
Fixtures, MSW route handlers and wagmi mocks exported "for reuse" that nothing imports — repo-wide grep finds only the definition, the README, and an archived agent prompt. Mechanical dead-code deletion rather than a structural defect, hence P2 not P1.
Cites:
- `packages/wallet-shared/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts:251-262,313-338,342-373` (`mockStoreActions`, `mockBackendAPI`, `mockWebAuthN`)
- `packages/wallet-shared/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts:271-311` (only `mockWagmiHooks.useConnection` is consumed)

Rework: delete the unused fixtures and mocks; leave the MSW handlers pending a check that no wallet test hits them passively.

### Slice: SW — `sdk/{core,react,components,legacy}` + `sdk/AGENTS.md`

**Coverage.** Read fully (~150 files): all `sdk/core/src` non-test sources, all 25 `sdk/react` sources, all `sdk/components` sources (except `.css.ts` bodies), all `sdk/legacy`, the 4 AGENTS.md, 4 READMEs, 4 `tsdown.config.ts`.
Read fully also: ~22 core tests, 9 react hook/provider tests, 6 components tests, both `tests/vitest-{fixtures,setup}.ts` sets.
Skimmed (partial/grep-driven): ~25 test files (`frakContext`, `iframeHelper`, `initFrakSdk`, `ButtonShare`, `PostPurchase`, `Banner`, `useReward`, …), the three `scripts/generate-golden-*.ts` headers, CHANGELOGs.
Not reached: ~30 test files (rewards/identity/context golden-fixture suites, `processReferral`, `setupClient`, `iframeLifecycleManager`, components `ButtonWallet.test.tsx`, `loader.test.ts`), 3 `vitest.config.ts`, golden JSON fixtures.

**Verification.** `confirmed 53 / adjusted 3 / rejected 3 / added 11 · trust H` — every claim the verifier opened was substantively real (file, construct and reasoning held in ~95% of cases); only #38 was a genuine classification error.
Line-number drift is systematic but cosmetic (typically +2 to −17 lines, e.g. `ButtonShare.tsx:126-129` → `:143-146`, `sso.ts:114` → `:120`); paths, symbols and content were always correct. Corrected lines are used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### SW-P1 — TEST — P1 — Core action tests rebuild an inline `FrakClient` mock per test and assert only that the mock was called (4 files per-test duplication; 15 files use the idiom, verifier count)
Four action test files rebuild `{config:{metadata:{name:"Test App"}}, request: vi.fn()} as unknown as FrakClient` inside every `it()` (35 literals), then assert `request` was called with the literal the one-line function forwards. "Propagate errors"/"network errors" pairs differ only by the error string — `displayModal.test.ts:325-347` is byte-for-byte `:303-323`. The `as any` sub-claim is void: `biome.json:101-118` exempts tests.
Cites:
- `sdk/core/src/actions/displayModal.test.ts:24-31, 303-323, 325-347`
- `sdk/core/src/actions/watchWalletStatus.test.ts:69-74, 92-97, 359-368`

##### SW-P2 — TEST — P1 — React hook tests re-test TanStack `useMutation` with the action fully mocked (4 files, verifier count 4)
Four hook tests carry the same four cases — mutateAsync, RPC errors, mutation options, reset state — against hooks that are one `useMutation({mutationFn: p => action(client, p)})`. Verified by reading two in full: `reset()` → `isIdle`, `onSuccess` fired, `isSuccess` flips — all TanStack's behaviour, not the wrapper's.
Cites:
- `sdk/react/src/hook/useDisplayModal.test.ts:160-275`
- `sdk/react/src/hook/useOpenSso.test.ts:115-200`
- `sdk/react/src/hook/useSendTransaction.test.ts:104-220`
- `sdk/react/src/hook/useSiweAuthenticate.test.ts:141-255`
Rework: one `describe.each` over hook/action pairs with two cases ("throws ClientNotFound", "forwards params"); delete the TanStack-behaviour cases.

##### SW-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — History narration in source comments (13 files auditor, 16 files verifier)
Comments explaining what the code used to do, what was retired, what "previously" broke. Root AGENTS.md names these verbs explicitly ("used to", "previously", "no longer", "the old X") and says the story belongs in the commit message — this is the decay the compass says keeps regressing. Verifier widened the set to include two test files.

- `sdk/core/src/types/resolvedConfig.ts:33-36, 39-42`
- `sdk/core/src/utils/iframe/iframeHelper.ts:101-104`

- `sdk/components/tsdown.config.ts:69, 282`
Rework: cut each to the surviving invariant; move the story to the CHANGELOG, where it already is.

`core/tests/vitest-fixtures.ts` declares 15 `test.extend` fixtures; only `mockUint8Arrays`/`mockBase64Strings` are ever destructured (in `b64.test.ts`) — 13 fixtures plus their JSDoc and `biome-ignore` lines are dead. React's `mockCoreActions`/`mockWalletStatus` have zero consumers, and its setup mocks the root `@frak-labs/core-sdk` module while all 18 hook sites import `/actions` — provably inert.

The compasses and READMEs document a removed class, wrong counts and wrong paths: `DebugInfoGatherer` appears only in docs and in the CHANGELOG entries recording its removal; "14 actions" vs 17 exported; "10 public hooks" vs 15; components' Key Files point at `src/utils/*` when the trees are `src/styles/`, `src/webcomponent/`, `src/bootstrap/`; `rpc.ts` names a non-existent `frak_sso` and a phantom `ResponseType` field.
