#### Rejected
- `auditor #38` — `sdk/core/src/utils/format/getSupportedCurrency.ts:10`, `getSupportedLocale.ts:12`, `getCurrencyAmountKey.ts:11` — the `if (!currency)` guards are load-bearing: without them you get `"undefinedAmount"` or a TS 7 error passing `Currency | undefined` to `in`/an index signature.
- `auditor #58` — `sdk/core/src/utils/cache/lruMap.ts:10` — not slop: adapted from viem, subclassing `Map` is the honest form, and the no-classes rule targets service/domain classes; the auditor itself recommended "accept".
- (`auditor #47` was rejected by the verifier on `biome.json:103-116`, but the cross-cutting test-file policy retains it at P3 — see SW-29.)

#### Checked and clean
- `sdk/core/src/identity/{canonical,sign,derive,types}.ts` — long docs, but they are the frozen wire-format spec shared with backend/native; comments are invariants.
- `sdk/core/src/rewards/*` (`select`, `format`, `conditions`, `example`, `operators`) — tight, typed, no dead exports; backend/wallet consume them.
- `sdk/core/src/utils/{url/queryParams,product/sanitizeProducts,i18n/detectPageLanguage,browser/inAppBrowser,browser/deepLinkWithFallback}.ts` — comments state real traps.
- `sdk/core/src/clients/createIFrameFrakClient.test.ts` — genuine race/ordering regression tests with meaningful assertions.
- `sdk/components/tsdown.config.ts` `vanillaExtractInlinePlugin` / `assertComponentRegistrations`; `sdk/components/src/components/{PostPurchase,Banner}.tsx` — justified complexity, real build gate, real impression gating.
- `sdk/legacy/**` pass-through and `sdk/AGENTS.md` — thin and accurate on build order, exports and subpaths (the `gapianne` specific is filed separately as SW-56).

### Slice: SN — `sdk/android/**` + `sdk/ios/**` + `scripts/comment-budget-baseline.json`

**Coverage.** Read fully: all 63 `frak-sdk` + 19 `frak-sdk-ui` Kotlin main files; 46/59 `FrakSDK` + 16/19 `FrakSDKUI` Swift files; `Package.swift`, both `scripts/run.sh`, `sdk/android/README.md`, both `CHANGELOG.md`, `scripts/check-comments.ts`, `scripts/comment-budget-baseline.json`.
Partial/grep-only: most Android/iOS test files (doc-block headers via grep), a handful of iOS leaf utilities (`Core/{Base64URL,Hex}`, `Net/{PercentEncoding,URLQuery}`, `Rewards/*`, `AppLink/*`).
Not reached: `sdk/ios/README.md`, `README.mirror.md`, `PRIVACY.md`, `buildSrc/**`, `*.gradle.kts`, `.api` dumps (verifier reached `buildSrc` and the iOS README — see SN-15, SN-30).

**Verification.** `confirmed 15 / adjusted 11 / rejected 0 (2 sub-claims dropped) / added 6 · trust H` — every re-opened finding existed at or within a few lines of its cite and the classification held; defects were mechanical, plus *under*-counting on two systemic patterns.
Line drift: 2–11 lines in ~6 cites (`ConfigStore.swift`, `Frak.kt`, `NativeShare.kt`, `SharingSessionBuilder.kt`, `SharingSheetLogic.swift`), and one cite off by ~85 lines (`EventOutbox.swift`); corrected below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### SN-P1 — COMMENT — P1 — Cross-platform comparison comments ("matching Android", "mirrors iOS") (36 source files, verifier count; ≥38 with tests)
AGENTS.md explicitly bans comparing with the other platform, but `scripts/check-comments.ts:31-44` (`HISTORY_PHRASES`) has no rule family for it, so the sites are uncounted by the baseline and survive indefinitely. This is rationale that belongs in `docs/plans/native-sdk/`, and it rots the moment one platform changes — several cites already point at code the other side removed.
Cites:
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDKUI/SharingSheetLogic.swift:573-575` (verifier-added: `heightFraction` "mirrored on the other platform")
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Core/Base64URL.swift:5` (verifier-added)
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Net/HTTPClient.swift:52-54`, `:218`, `:236`
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDKUI/SharingResult.swift:18` ("Spelled identically on Android")
- `sdk/android/frak-sdk/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/core/FrakError.kt:15`, `:52`
- `sdk/android/frak-sdk-ui/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/ui/NativeShare.kt:56-57`
Rework: add a `cross-platform` regex family to `check-comments.ts` and pay these down, keeping only wire-contract facts.

##### SN-P2 — COMMENT — P1 — History narration that dodges the linter's phrase list (11 files, verifier count)
The linter catches `used to/previously/no longer/the old`; the same content survives as "the deleted X", "owns the stream now", "replaces Y", "was dropped in <sha>". Two references are already dangling — `InteractionTracker` has exactly one repo-wide hit, the comment itself — and one describes a state that ended when `check:native-versions` shipped.
Cites:
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/DefaultFrakClient.swift:223` (only referent is itself), `:206`, `:371`
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Config/ConfigStore.swift:63-69`, `:72-75` (corrected lines)
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDKUI/SharingTrace.swift:7-8` (raw sha `c863486df`)
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/FrakSDKVersion.swift:2` (stale vs `scripts/native-version.ts:75,82`)
- `sdk/android/frak-sdk/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/tracking/RowSender.kt:24-27`; `sdk/android/frak-sdk-ui/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/ui/SharingSheetState.kt:333-336`
Rework: extend `HISTORY_PHRASES` with `the deleted`, `was dropped in`, `owns .* now`, `\b[0-9a-f]{7,}\b`; delete the narration, keep the invariant.

##### SN-P3 — TEST — P1 — Ticket/finding labels baked into test names and test comments (6 files, verifier count)
Test names and doc comments carry opaque review-item identifiers ("(C3)", "(9.3)", "(bug 1)", "(S3)") with no anchor in the repo, and one suite carries a 7-line paragraph on what it deliberately does *not* cover — the exact shape AGENTS.md forbids. The test name is supposed to be the documentation; "(bug 2)" documents nothing.
Cites:
- `sdk/ios/Tests/FrakSDKTests/Config/ConfigStoreTests.swift:101`, `:162-168`, `:417`
- `sdk/android/frak-sdk/src/test/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/config/ConfigStoreTest.kt:101`, `:132`, `:347` (verifier-added, missed by auditor)
- `sdk/ios/Tests/FrakSDKTests/Tracking/EventQueueTests.swift:492` (verifier-added, "(S3)")
- `sdk/ios/Tests/FrakSDKTests/Tracking/EventOutboxTests.swift:315`, `:327`
- `sdk/ios/Tests/FrakSDKTests/FrakClientTests.swift:604`, `:624`, `:643`
Rework: rename tests to state the behaviour; delete the "no deterministic test here" paragraph or make it a disabled case.

##### SN-P4 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Identical tiny helpers copy-pasted within one platform (10 files, verifier count)
The same helper exists twice under a different name inside one platform tree. The Android `MainThreadDispatcher` pair is the indefensible one — same module family, same body, and *both* copies carry their own "hand-rolled because kotlinx-coroutines-android…" doc; the iOS pairs at least cross the `FrakSDK`/`FrakSDKUI` boundary, which is mitigating but not exculpating. Verifier dropped `CompletionFlag` from the latch group.
Cites:
- `sdk/android/frak-sdk/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/core/MainThreadDispatcher.kt:11-29` vs `sdk/android/frak-sdk-ui/src/main/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/ui/SharingHost.kt:578-597`
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDKUI/NativeShare.swift:147-160` vs `SharingImagePreview.swift:139-152` (same module, byte-identical)
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Net/HTTPClient.swift:5-14` vs `SharingImagePreview.swift:79-88`
- `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Net/Deadline.swift:10-25` vs `SharingImagePreview.swift:57-72`
- 4× copied `@file:OptIn` header comment: `net/UrlQuery.kt:1-2`, `net/HttpClient.kt:1-2`, `applink/InstallLinks.kt:1`, `frak-sdk-ui/.../SharingPageUrl.kt:1`
Rework: Android — expose core's dispatcher `@InternalFrakApi` and delete the UI copy; iOS — one `OnceLatch`, one `NoRedirectDelegate`, one `Deadline` behind `@_spi(FrakInternal)`.

#### Rejected

- `auditor #23b` — `sdk/android/frak-sdk-ui/src/test/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/ui/SharingPageUrlTest.kt:85-107` — not a test of a non-feature: `SharingPageUrl.kt:43` states the "no presentation params" invariant, so the test is its regression guard.
- `auditor #4 (member)` — `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDK/Config/SingleFlight.swift:5-19` — `CompletionFlag` is a set-once published flag (`set()`/`isSet`), not a first-claim-wins latch; merging it would change semantics.
- `auditor open question` — `sdk/android/frak-sdk/src/test/kotlin/id/frak/sdk/net/HttpClientTest.kt:32-33` — header is accurate, not stale: gzip/`Accept-Encoding` assertion at `:210`, `errorStream` at `:200`.
- `auditor open question` — `sdk/ios/Sources/FrakSDKUI/FrakSharingSheet.swift:57-58,68,123,148` — file contains zero `Android`/`Kotlin` mentions; those lines are on-topic SwiftUI traps (`SharingResult.swift:18` is a genuine SN-P1 site and is cited there).

#### Checked and clean

- All 53 `catch (` sites in `sdk/android` main rethrow `CancellationException`, translate to `FrakError`/`DeliveryOutcome`, or are documented best-effort degrades; `frakCall` (`DefaultFrakClient.kt:446-455`) is the single, consistently used normalisation boundary.
- Cross-platform duplication of codecs/queues (`FrakContextCodec`, `EventQueue`/`EventOutbox`, `Backoff`, `ConfigStore`, `RewardRepository`) is forced by two languages plus one wire format, and is pinned by the golden-fixture corpus — not slop.
- Hand-written `equals/hashCode/toString` and the Builder-per-input-type shape on the Android read models are deliberate ABI decisions documented at `sdk/android/README.md:145-165`.
- `SharingDependencies` (Android) and the closure-bag injection in `SharingSheetModel.init` (iOS, `:130-175`) are legitimate test seams with one prod implementation each; `DeliveryOutcome`/`RowSender`/`RowSenders` has three real participants per platform.
- Tight single-purpose utilities with trap-only comments: Android `MainSafeFuture`, `ServerClock`, `UrlQuery`, `PercentEncoding`, `Base64Url`, `Hex`; iOS `FrakCall`, `RowBody`, `SendContext`, `InstallProbeSchedule`, `QueriedSchemes`, `FrakStorage`, `MerchantQuery`, `Backoff`.
- No `TODO`/`FIXME` rot anywhere in either SDK; `#expect(Bool(true))` is the single tautology in ~90 test files; `PublicSurfaceTests.swift` is a load-bearing stand-in for Android's ABI dump and guards its own loop against vacuous truth (`:176`).

### Slice: LS — `apps/listener/**` + `apps/shopify/**`

**Coverage.** Read in full: listener `bootstrap.ts`, `uiBus/uiRuntime/queryClient/i18nOverrideQueue`, `vite.config.ts`, `ui/ListenerUiProvider.tsx`, `module/{handlers,middleware,stores,hooks,utils,queryKeys}` plus 4 hook tests and `Modal/index.tsx`; shopify all `AGENTS.md`, `routes/*` (11 files), `services.server/{backendMerchant,purchase,purchase.helpers,metafields(partial)}`, `utils/{onboarding,env}`, `hooks/*`, `components/{Stepper,ui,Skeleton,Sharing}`.
Grep-only: shopify `services.server/{merchant,theme,shop,webhook,webPixel,requestId,logger}` + tests, `components/{Appearance,Funding}`, `extensions/**`, `db/**`; listener `module/sharing/**`, other modal components, `i18n*`.
Not reached: shopify `drizzle/**`, `server.js`, `Dockerfile`, `*.toml`, locale JSON; listener `*.css.ts`, `tests/fixtures.ts`.

**Verification.** `confirmed 25 / adjusted 8 / rejected 0 / added 9 · trust H` — every claim the verifier spot-opened (~60 cites across 45 files) was materially true, including the `"prod"` stage bug and the inert `vi.mock` specifier, both independently re-derived.
Line drift: three files drifted (worst `Modal/index.tsx`, ~35 lines early); also `vite.config.ts:459→463`, `routes/AGENTS.md:84→76`, `purchase.ts` ~6 lines — corrected cites used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### LS-P1 — DOCS — P1 — Every Shopify `AGENTS.md` plus the listener compass documents files and symbols that do not exist (8 doc artifacts, verifier count)
Six compasses (+ one root line + one vendored CHANGELOG) describe a directory layout, file inventory and API names that never matched or no longer match the tree: `api.mint.tsx`, `getProductSetupCode`, `mint.ts`, `utils/abis/campaignAbis.ts`, `ModalPreview/`, `SocialPreview/`, `WalletGated/`, CSS Modules, a README that does not exist, counts that are off (11 vs 12 services, 4 vs 5 hooks, 17 vs 20 routes, 6- vs 7-step wizard). These are the primary onboarding artifact and are cited by the root compass, so an agent following them looks for files that were never there.
Cites:
- `apps/listener/AGENTS.md:13-15` (`app/entry.client.tsx`, `app/views/`, `app/module/providers/` — none exist)
- `apps/shopify/AGENTS.md:37` (README "lies" about a README that does not exist)
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/AGENTS.md:21` and `:76` (`api.mint.tsx`, `getProductSetupCode`)
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/AGENTS.md:38` and `:3` (`mint.ts`; "11 service files", there are 12)
- `apps/shopify/app/components/AGENTS.md:8-28` (`ModalPreview/`, `SocialPreview/`, `WalletGated/`, `Step1–6`)
Rework: re-derive each compass from the tree once, delete the `ls`-mirroring inventory tables, and delete the vendored `apps/shopify/CHANGELOG.md`.

##### LS-P2 — DEAD — P1 — Exported API kept alive only by its own unit test (6 source files, 13 dead exports, verifier count)
A set of app-internal exports has exactly two references: the definition and a `describe` block; two listener selectors are byte-identical duplicates of selectors that are used. The tests therefore assert nothing about the product while blocking any dead-code pass.
Cites:
- `apps/listener/app/module/stores/modalStore.ts:60`, `:76`, `:89` (`completeStep`/`nextStep`/`setDismissed`, test-only)
- `apps/listener/app/module/stores/modalStore.ts:144` `selectActiveStep` (identical to `:197`, which is used at `modal/component/Generic/index.tsx:23`)
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/backendMerchant.ts:179`, `:221`, `:503` (create/publish campaign, unused return type)
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/metafields.ts:1076` `getKlaviyoShareMetafields` (doc claims the appearance loader returns it; it does not)
- `apps/listener/app/i18nOverrideQueue.ts:87` `_resetI18nOverrideQueueForTests` (`todo: to be deleted` at `:85`)
Rework: delete each export together with its `describe` block; collapse the duplicated selector pairs and fix the imports in `Modal/index.tsx`.

##### LS-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — Comment blocks over budget, narrating mechanism, or recording history (≥20 source files, verifier count)
The rule is ≤5 lines per block, never longer than the code, never history, never mechanism. All clauses break repeatedly: 21-line JSDoc on a formatter with a fabricated perf claim and an `@example` for a middleware that does not exist, 11-line doc on a 2-line barrel, and a verifier grep for `used to|previously|no longer|the old|originally` returning 17 source files. TS is not gated by `lint:comments`, and the tree shows it.
Cites:
- `apps/listener/app/module/middleware/logging.ts:12-33` (21 lines, `compressionMiddleware` example, "console.log is async")
- `apps/listener/app/module/middleware/index.ts:1-11` (11-line doc on a 2-line barrel)
- `apps/listener/app/bootstrap.ts:8`, `:117`, `:192` (history)
- `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useSdkCleanup.ts:19,25,29,32` (narration over `localStorage.clear()`)
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/api.purchase.tsx:16` ("including auth" — auth already ran at `:13`)
Adjusted: the "cross-platform comparison" label on `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useDeepLinkFallback.ts:21-24` is retagged DUPLICATION/P3 — the trap knowledge is genuine, the slop is repeating the same sentence at `:5-11`, `:16-24`, `:30-32`.
Rework: delete history and narration outright; move the one genuinely long rationale (vite chunking) to `docs/plans/**` and point `lint:comments` at both `app/` trees.

##### LS-P4 — COMMENT — P2 — ASCII section-banner comments used as file structure (11 files, 43 banner pairs, verifier count)
`/* ---- */` and `// ====` banner pairs segment long files and test files instead of splitting the module; the banner text usually restates the `describe()` on the next line. It is the tell that a file outgrew its structure — `metafields.ts` is 1085 lines with 8 pairs.
Cites:
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/metafields.ts:17-19, 337-339, 467-469, 503-505, 537-539, 885-887, 921-923, 984-986`
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/backendMerchant.ts:28-30, 79-81, 93-95`
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/backendMerchant.test.ts:27-29, 35-37, 172-174, 344-346, 466-468`
- `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useWalletStatusListener.test.ts:3-6, 35-42, 71-73`
- `apps/listener/vite.config.ts:437-439, 486-488, 585-587`
(The auditor's 12th cite, `Modal/index.css.ts:32`, is a single divider, not a pair.)
Rework: delete the banners; split `metafields.ts` where a banner separates genuinely unrelated concerns.

##### LS-P5 — TYPE — P1 — `interface` used for plain object types against the monorepo rule (12 files, 15 declarations, verifier count)
The rule is `type` over `interface` except for declaration merging, and four Shopify compasses restate it — next to code that breaks it fifteen times. A rule stated four times and unenforced everywhere is doc filler.
Cites:
- `apps/listener/app/module/stores/types.ts:74`, `:93`
- `apps/shopify/app/components/ui/Tabs.tsx:3` and `:8`, `DescriptionList.tsx:4` and `:9`
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/theme.ts:327`, `apps/shopify/db/adapter/sessionAdapter.ts:14`
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/auth.login/error.server.tsx:4`
- Rule restated at `apps/shopify/app/routes/AGENTS.md:69`, `services.server/AGENTS.md:49`, `hooks/AGENTS.md:41`, `components/AGENTS.md:44`
(`apps/shopify/app/globals.d.ts:8` is legitimate declaration merging, excluded.)
Rework: mechanical `interface X {` → `type X = {`, then keep the rule statement once at the root.

##### LS-P6 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Copy-pasted service/handler bodies differing only by a method name and a log string (6 files, verifier count)
Six Shopify campaign-lifecycle functions are the same 25-line shell (resolve → guard → try → call → `levelForStatus` log → cache-delete → return; catch → `log.error` → null) with the endpoint segment and two strings swapped; the same catch shell appears 17 times in that one file. The two listener `impl` handlers duplicate placement resolution and the emitter closure verbatim, and three routes repeat a per-promise catch shape.
Cites:
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/backendMerchant.ts:179, 221, 249, 285, 321, 357` (create/publish/pause/resume/archive/delete)
- `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useDisplayModalListener.impl.ts:119-127, 126-142` vs `useDisplaySharingPageListener.impl.ts:45-53, 70-87`
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/app.settings.pixel.tsx:11-38` vs `app.settings.webhook.tsx:11-61`
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/app.appearance.tsx:58-100` (8 consecutive `.catch` blocks) and `app.settings.tsx:45-83` (same shape)
Rework: extract `campaignTransition(...)` + `callBackend(label, fn)` in `backendMerchant.ts`, hoist `resolvePlacement`/`makeDeferredEmitter` into a shared listener module, and add one `logged(label, fallback, promise)` helper for the three routes.

#### Rejected
- None.

#### Checked and clean
- `apps/listener/app/uiBus.ts` — single-purpose, correct queue semantics; the doc block is trap knowledge (Ring 0 dependency ban).
- `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useWalletStatusListener.ts` — comments are genuine invariants (abort-after-await, "server is sole authority"); abort handling is deliberate.
- `apps/listener/app/module/hooks/useOnGetMergeToken.ts` and its test — tight; comments explain wire-format quirks, not mechanism.
- `apps/listener/app/queryClient.ts`, `app/i18nOverrideQueue.ts` — long headers, minimal code, coherent lazy-hydration design.
- `apps/shopify/app/routes/{health,_index}.tsx`, `app/components/ui/{ExternalLink,SkeletonBodyText,SkeletonDisplayText,SkeletonPage}.tsx`, `app/components/Skeleton/index.tsx` — small, single-purpose, no filler.
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/purchase.helpers.ts` and `app/utils/onboarding.ts:60-92` — real pure functions / genuine domain logic; `routes/webhooks.tsx:13-18` "NEVER log the raw payload" is a model trap comment.

---

### Slice: PI — `plugins/**`, `infra/**` + `sst.config.ts`, `scripts/**`, `services/{bootstrap,credential-sync}`, root configs, `.github/workflows/**`

**Coverage.** Read fully: all 16 `scripts/**` TS files; root `biome.json`/`knip.ts`/`vitest.config.ts`/`tsconfig.json`/`sst.config.ts`; all of `infra/` (`utils`, `config`, `example`, both components, 10 `gcp/*`); all of `services/{bootstrap,credential-sync}` src + build; 5 `.github` workflow/action files; core files of all three PHP plugins (magento `PLAN.md` 1550 lines, prestashop classes, wordpress widgets/blocks).
Grep-only (not cited): remaining prestashop `classes/*`, `controllers/`, `upgrade/`, `sql/`; remaining wordpress `includes/*`, `admin/views/`; magento `Block/{ShareButton,WalletButton,OpenInApp,PurchaseTracker}` + `view/**`.
Not reached: 7 other workflows (`apps.yaml`, `release*.yml`, `tauri-mobile-release.yml`, native SDK releases), `infra/gcp-tunnel.sh` (verifier reached it), `infra/monitoring/dashboards/*.json`, `services/bootstrap/drizzle/**` (generated), plugin `i18n/`, `composer.lock`, `.distignore`.

**Verification.** `confirmed 33 / adjusted 9 / rejected 0 / added 8 · trust H` — verifier re-opened every cited `path:line`, found the overwhelming majority byte-exact, and confirmed all "absent file" claims (`Dockerfile.base`, `etc/db_schema.xml`, `etc/crontab.xml`, wordpress `phpunit.xml`).
Line drift: never more than ~15 lines and always on the right construct; the worst is finding #12's fourth guard (`:358-362`, not `:344-349`); one-to-two-line drift on #1, #14, #17, #19, #25, #28, #41 — corrected cites used below.

#### Systemic patterns

##### PI-P1 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Three copy-pasted "pin-site gate" scripts plus a cloned `die()`/`read()` prelude (7 files, verifier count 7)
`check-bun-version.ts`, `check-ios-floor.ts` and `native-version.ts` are the same program three times: a `type Site` carrying the *same* doc sentence verbatim, a `SITES` table, and structurally identical `die`/`read`/`extract` (verifier: the `extract` logic is character-identical, only the `die` copy differs). `die()` is re-declared in 7 scripts, `read()` in 5, so a fix to the `values` count semantics must be made three times and a new gate is written by copy-paste instead of by adding a table.
Cites:
- `scripts/check-bun-version.ts:13`, `scripts/check-ios-floor.ts:15`, `scripts/native-version.ts:11` (identical `Site` doc sentence)
- `scripts/check-bun-version.ts:19`, `scripts/check-ios-floor.ts:21`, `scripts/native-version.ts:17`
- `scripts/check-bun-version.ts:91`, `scripts/check-ios-floor.ts:105`, `scripts/native-version.ts:115` (identical `extract`)
- `scripts/check-android-manifest.ts:19`, `scripts/check-es-version.ts:65`, `scripts/check-safe-area.ts:44` (`die` copies)
Rework: one `scripts/lib/gate.ts` exporting `die`, `read`, `Site`, `extract`, `checkSites(truth, sites, label)`; the three gates collapse to a table plus one call.

##### PI-P2 — DUPLICATION — P1 — `services/bootstrap` re-declares its Postgres URL builder and migration routing four times (5 files, verifier count 5)
`buildPostgresUrl()`/`buildDatabaseUrl()` is written out four times with the same five `process.env.POSTGRES_*` reads, and the `local` → `_v2` → prod/dev migration-folder routing is written twice with a comment admitting the two must be hand-synced. A step that forgets one env var, or a routing rule that changes, silently connects to the wrong schema. The S3 client construction is a third copy.
Cites:
- `services/bootstrap/src/migrate-pg.ts:30` / `:31`, `services/bootstrap/drizzle.config.ts:20` / `:21`
- `services/bootstrap/src/backfill-auth-bindings.ts:260`, `services/bootstrap/src/backfill-business-accounts.ts:176`
- `services/bootstrap/src/migrate-pg.ts:5-8` ("Keep this in sync with …drizzle.config.ts") vs `services/bootstrap/drizzle.config.ts:5-18`
- `services/bootstrap/src/ensure-buckets.ts:72-79` vs `services/bootstrap/src/backfill-image-variants.ts:45-52` (identical `S3Client`)
Rework: one `src/env.ts` exporting `postgresUrl()`, `migrationTarget()`, `s3Client()`; `drizzle.config.ts` imports the routing instead of restating it.

##### PI-P3 — COMMENT — P1 — Cross-platform "Mirrors the WordPress sibling / Magento's X" comments across the PHP plugins (verifier count 19 files, ≥25 counting intra-plugin mirrors)
PrestaShop and WordPress narrate each other (and Magento) in source: "Mirrors `Frak_Component_Renderer` in the WordPress plugin", "Kept parallel with …", "unlike WP, which has to …". Root `AGENTS.md` explicitly bans cross-platform comparison comments; these claims are unverifiable from the file you are reading and rot the moment a sibling changes — the consistency they assert is already broken (see PI-29).
Cites:
- `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakComponentRenderer.php:11`, `:59`, `:168`, `:219-220`
- `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakUtils.php:6`, `:44`; `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakConfig.php:18`
- `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakMerchantResolver.php:26`, `:35`; `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakInstaller.php:25-26`
- `plugins/prestashop/frakintegration.php:25`, `:209`; `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-funnel-compat.php:11`
Rework: delete the comparisons; where parity matters (attribute maps, HMAC encoding, backoff schedule) assert it with a test or shared fixture.

##### PI-P4 — COMMENT — P1 — History narration in source across infra, scripts and plugins (verifier count 16 files)
Comments recording what the code *used to* be — the exact shape root `AGENTS.md` bans and `scripts/check-comments.ts` enforces on `.kt`/`.swift`. It is commit-message content living where nobody deletes it, and several instances are already wrong (see PI-2, PI-5).
Cites:
- `scripts/check-comments.ts:21` (matched by the file's own `HISTORY_PHRASES` at `:33`)
- `infra/gcp/images.ts:7` (11-line block, `:5-15`); `sst.config.ts:71-72`
- `infra/gcp/shopify.ts:164`, `:173`
- `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakInfra.php:6`, `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakHttpClient.php:29`, `plugins/prestashop/classes/FrakUtils.php:28`
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-wc-webhook-registrar.php:6`, `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-plugin.php:72`, `plugins/wordpress/admin/class-frak-admin.php:102`
Rework: delete each; where the removed alternative is a real trap, restate the constraint in one line — and consider extending `bun run lint:comments` to `scripts/**` and `infra/**`.

##### PI-P5 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Each WordPress component's control schema is declared on five surfaces (verifier count 15 sites: 5 surfaces × 3 components)
For share-button, banner and post-purchase the same attributes, labels, help text and defaults are written in `block.json`, `blocks/*/editor.js` (the fifth surface the auditor missed), the classic widget, the Elementor widget and the Divi module. Drift is already merchant-visible: the same field carries different defaults and different help text per surface.
Cites:
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/blocks/share-button/block.json:18-25` (default `"primary"`)
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-share-button-widget.php:37-73`, help at `:42` ("the **live** reward amount", no default)
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-elementor-share-button-widget.php:90` ("at runtime")
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/divi/class-frak-divi-share-button-module.php:64-109` (snake_case keys)
- `plugins/wordpress/includes/blocks/share-button/editor.js:68`
Rework: one PHP schema array per component that all five surfaces project from; adapters keep only per-builder control-type mapping.

##### PI-P6 — ABSTRACTION/DEAD — P1 — The Magento webhook-retry layer's runtime prerequisites do not exist (4 impl files + di.xml + phpcs.xml.dist + 1 test)
An interface, a resolver and two strategies select between AMQP and a DB-backed cron queue, but neither backend can run: `CronRetry` writes to `fraklabs_webhook_queue` with no `db_schema.xml` and no `Setup/`; `processRetries()` has no `etc/crontab.xml`; `MessageQueueRetry::processRetries()` is intentionally empty and there is no queue topology. On any store without RabbitMQ the enqueue is a raw insert into a missing table, inside the catch protecting checkout. Tests assert against a mocked interface, so nothing catches it.
Cites:
- `plugins/magento/Model/Retry/CronRetry.php:17`; `plugins/magento/PLAN.md:1341`, `:1513`
- `plugins/magento/Model/Retry/MessageQueueRetry.php:46` (`// Intentionally empty`)
- `plugins/magento/etc/di.xml:4-10`; `plugins/magento/phpcs.xml.dist:11-13`
- `plugins/magento/Observer/OrderPlaceAfterObserver.php:78-93`; `plugins/magento/Test/Unit/Observer/OrderPlaceAfterObserverTest.php:139`, `:182`
Rework: finish it (`db_schema.xml`, `crontab.xml`, `queue_*.xml`, an integration test that inserts and drains) or delete the layer and let the observer log — the backend reconciles by `(order id, protectCode)`.

##### PI-P7 — DOCS — P1 — AGENTS.md compasses in this slice describe files, events and commands that do not exist (verifier count 5 docs, 7 distinct false claims)
Four-plus compasses assert concrete facts the code contradicts; each wrong line is a wrong agent action.
Cites:
- `infra/AGENTS.md:19` names `isV2` — `infra/utils.ts` exports only `isGcp:4`, `isProd:9`, `normalizedStageName:15`; `infra/AGENTS.md:20` names a `Dockerfile.base` that does not exist
- `services/bootstrap/AGENTS.md` lists 3 steps; `services/bootstrap/src/index.ts:23-28` runs 6, and `services/bootstrap/src/ensure-buckets.ts:63-66` creates `billing-` too
- `plugins/magento/AGENTS.md:18-19` names `etc/{webapi,crontab,queue*}.xml` and `sales_order_place_after` — `plugins/magento/etc/events.xml:5`, `:10`, `:15` hook different events; `:3` vs `plugins/magento/composer.json:2`, `:14`
- `plugins/wordpress/AGENTS.md` runs `vendor/bin/phpunit` and claims a sender — `plugins/wordpress/includes/class-frak-wc-webhook-registrar.php:5-17` delegates to WooCommerce; `.github/workflows/php-plugins.yaml:53`, `:66` have no WP test job
- `plugins/prestashop/AGENTS.md` Release Flow vs `.github/workflows/release-php-plugins.yml:5-19`, `:108-113`
Rework: re-derive each Key Files / Quick Commands block from the tree at review time, or drop the enumerations and keep only the non-obvious patterns.

#### Rejected
- None. All 42 auditor findings and all 7 systemic patterns survived independent verification of cited file, line and content.

#### Checked and clean
- `biome.json` — the listener/`wallet-shared` subpath rule, the global scope rule and the charts complexity exemption are deliberate and match root `AGENTS.md`; messages are specific and actionable.
- `tsconfig.json` root config — minimal, no dead paths; `exclude: ["node_modules", "plugins/**"]` matches the plugins' toolchain isolation.
- `.github/workflows/release-php-plugins.yml` + `.github/actions/release-plugin-{bump,publish}/action.yml` — the composite-action extraction is exactly the factoring `php-plugins.yaml` lacks; version-file `case` handling is shared, not copied.
- `scripts/check-es-version.ts` / `check-publishable.ts` + their tests — the `why` field is actually printed in failures, exported pure functions exist for the tests that use them, and the tests assert real behaviour; `scripts/bun.d.ts` `interface ImportMeta` is allowed declaration merging.
- `services/credential-sync/src/{sqld,mongo,types}.ts`, `infra/gcp/{secrets,sandbox,credential-sync}.ts`, `infra/example.ts` — small, single-purpose, no dead exports; env grouping genuinely navigational. (Verifier re-opened `infra/gcp/dev.ts` and found PI-50 there.)
- `plugins/magento/Model/Config.php` — the `getEnvironment()`/`isEnvironmentHalfConfigured()` pairing is a real invariant with real consumers (`Block/SdkLoader.php:95-107`, `Model/WebhookSender.php:36`) and a config-XML cross-check test; the WordPress widget base-class split delegating to `Frak_Component_Renderer` is the right shape.

### Slice: DM — root `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md`/`README.md`, all child `AGENTS.md`, READMEs, `docs/**`, CHANGELOGs, `.claude/**`, `.cursor/**`, `.opencode/**`

**Coverage.** Read fully: root `AGENTS.md`/`CLAUDE.md`/`README.md`/`opencode.json`/`.changeset/*`, 17 of 25 child `AGENTS.md`, 14 READMEs, 10 `docs/**` files (some partial), 3 CHANGELOGs, and the `.claude`/`.cursor`/`.opencode` meta files listed in the raw report; ~30 code spot-checks to verify claims.
Grep-only: remaining `.claude/agents/*`, `.opencode/agent/*` bodies, five `.cursor/rules/*.mdc`, `docs/graphs.md:51-246`, `.cursor/rules/013-testing.mdc:46-574`, changeset-generated CHANGELOGs.
Not reached: `plugins/prestashop/README.md` and `tauri-plugin-frak-firebase/README.md` (both re-opened by the verifier), `sdk/{android,ios}` READMEs + `sdk/android/CHANGELOG.md`, several `docs/audits/*` and `docs/plans/*`, `services/backend/docs/product-scoped-campaigns.md`.
Excluded: `example/**` READMEs, generated Tauri permission reference `.md`.

**Verification.** `confirmed 21 / adjusted 10 / rejected 0 / added 10 · trust H` — every systemic pattern and every re-opened factual claim held up under 15+ independent code spot-checks; corrections are line drift, two overstated tallies, and systematic *under*-counting of pattern extent.
Line-number drift: verifier corrected #19 (`:237`, not `:266`), #20 (25–40 lines low), #21 (sibling cite `:134-137`), #22 (`:9`), #26 (`:162-178`), #29 (`:59`), #31 (±2), and PATTERN-1's `002-frontends.mdc:61`.

#### Systemic patterns

##### DM-P1 — DOCS — P1 — "CSS Modules" prescribed as the styling system while the repo has zero `.module.css` (14 files auditor, 15 verifier; 16 counting `packages/AGENTS.md:29`)
Root `AGENTS.md:46` and `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md:29` declare the CSS-Modules migration complete and `.module.css` forbidden; `catalog_find '**/*.module.css'` returns no files. Yet every AI-instruction surface still mandates CSS Modules, several with copy-paste `import styles from "./index.module.css"` examples. These are the files an agent reads before touching frontend code, so they actively produce anti-pattern violations rather than merely being stale.
Cites:
- `.cursor/rules/021-sdk-components.mdc:12,27` (verifier-added instance)
- `.cursor/rules/002-frontends.mdc:23,41,61,75`
- `.opencode/agent/frontend-builder.md:16,25,33`
- `apps/business/README.md:22,138,145`
- `CLAUDE.md:218`
- `apps/shopify/app/components/AGENTS.md:22,23,37`
Rework: replace CSS Modules with Vanilla Extract `.css.ts` + `Box` sprinkles across `.claude/`, `.cursor/`, `.opencode/`, `apps/business/README.md`, `CLAUDE.md` — or delete the redundant instruction surfaces (DM-P5).

##### DM-P2 — DOCS — P1 — Vitest project count stated five different ways, none correct (6 files auditor, 7 docs + root config verifier)
Ground truth is 15 project configs discovered by glob at `vitest.config.ts:37-53`; the config's own header enumerates 13. Docs variously claim 7, 10, or "12 of 13", and the 7-variant enumerates a list omitting shopify, design-system, app-essentials, dev-tooling, ui-preview and rpc — an agent reading it concludes those packages have no tests. A hand-maintained count of globbed directories duplicated across seven files, each drifting independently. The "(shopify is standalone)" parenthetical is also false.
Cites:
- `.claude/skills/code-quality/SKILL.md:34` (verifier-added instance)
- `CLAUDE.md:60` and `CLAUDE.md:84`
- `packages/test-foundation/README.md:3,7-14`
- `packages/AGENTS.md:20`
- `.cursor/rules/013-testing.mdc:14,71`
Rework: state "one project per `vitest.config.ts`, discovered by glob" once in `packages/test-foundation/AGENTS.md`; delete every number.

##### DM-P3 — DOCS — P1 — Hand-maintained inventories of directories/exports that have all drifted (11 files auditor, 18 verifier)
A repeated shape across the AGENTS.md/README family: a bullet or table enumerating every file, module, hook, action or component in a directory, plus a count. Every instance re-derived by the verifier is wrong — deleted items still listed, added items missing. Highest-maintenance, lowest-value documentation form: it duplicates `ls`, adds nothing, and rots on the next commit; several entries name deliberately deleted symbols, sending readers hunting for dead code.
Cites:
- `AGENTS.md:26` "13 modules" vs 24 dirs in `apps/wallet/app/module/`
- `packages/wallet-shared/AGENTS.md:3,6` — `walletStore` does not exist; `detachedPairingSessionStore` undocumented
- `sdk/react/AGENTS.md:3,6` — "10 public hooks" vs 15 exported from `sdk/react/src/hook/index.ts`
- `apps/shopify/app/services.server/AGENTS.md:3,23-34,52-64` — `mint.ts`/`getProductSetupCode` do not exist
- `services/backend/AGENTS.md:3,18-19` — "12 domains" vs 16
- `packages/AGENTS.md:15` / `CLAUDE.md:185` — "28 components" vs 58 dirs
Rework: delete the enumerations and counts; keep only entries carrying non-obvious information (generated files, pure modules, foundations).

##### DM-P4 — DOCS — P1 — `CLAUDE.md` duplicates `AGENTS.md`'s job at 4× the length and is wrong wherever they overlap (2 files)
`CLAUDE.md` (~356 lines) and `AGENTS.md` (84 lines) both claim to be the repo compass; `AGENTS.md` declares itself non-obvious-knowledge-only, `CLAUDE.md` is the generic pass. Two compasses means an agent gets whichever it loads first. `CLAUDE.md` carries marketing copy, `package.json` script listings, tutorial prose, and a Change Log whose last entry is 2026-04-09 — unmaintained for five months while `AGENTS.md` moved.
Cites:
- `CLAUDE.md:218` vs `AGENTS.md:46` (CSS Modules)
- `CLAUDE.md:226` vs `AGENTS.md:72` (`BROWSER_TARGET` location)
- `CLAUDE.md:233` MongoDB — only `services/credential-sync` uses it
- `CLAUDE.md:183` AlertDialog duplication — single repo-wide hit is the doc itself
- `CLAUDE.md:351-356` Change Log "6 agents" vs 8 in `.claude/agents/`
Rework: delete `CLAUDE.md` and point Claude Code at `AGENTS.md`; everything unique to it is a script listing, a tutorial, or wrong.

##### DM-P5 — DEAD — P1 — Four parallel agent-instruction trees, mutually inconsistent and largely stale (~40 files auditor, 39 verifier; `.cursor` is 10 not 11)
`.claude/` (8 agents + 2 skills + 1 output-style), `.cursor/rules/` (10), `.opencode/` (7 active + 8 `_archive/` + 2 instructions), `.agents/skills/` (1) cover the same ground with conflicting content. Beyond DM-P1/DM-P2 they reference apps that do not exist, mandate libraries that are not installed, keep an explicitly-archived subtree in-tree, and open with generated "You are an elite…" persona filler.
Cites:
- `.cursor/rules/002-frontends.mdc:13` and `.claude/agents/react-performance-expert.md:3` — `apps/dashboard-admin`
- `.cursor/rules/002-frontends.mdc:22,37,41,75` + `012-frontend-wallet.mdc:10,24` — Shadcn UI, not installed
- `.cursor/rules/001-monorepo.mdc:19` — Typedoc, deleted as uninvoked per `AGENTS.md:56`
- `.opencode/agent/_archive/testing-specialist.md:24,85,122,123` — the `test-setup/` tree is gone
- `.claude/skills/frak-orchestrator/SKILL.md:29-33` — branches on a nonexistent `_workspace/`
Rework: pick one surface — keep the `AGENTS.md` family, delete `.cursor/rules/`, `.opencode/agent/_archive/`, the persona files, and reduce the rest to pointers.

##### DM-P6 — DUPLICATION — P1 — Boilerplate sentences copy-pasted verbatim across sibling docs (8 files)
Identical sentences repeated byte-for-byte in sibling files, each restating a rule already stated in the root compass or the parent doc and already enforced by Biome. One of the repeated blocks is a published-package intro carrying two typos, so the duplication is merchant-facing.
Cites:
- `apps/shopify/app/components/AGENTS.md:43`, `hooks/AGENTS.md:36`, `routes/AGENTS.md:86`, `services.server/AGENTS.md:49`, `extensions/AGENTS.md:89` — "Types over interfaces", also at root `AGENTS.md:78`
- `sdk/core/README.md:3,5`, `sdk/react/README.md:3,5`, `sdk/components/README.md:7` — same intro, typos included
- `apps/shopify/AGENTS.md:35`, `components/AGENTS.md:83`, `routes/AGENTS.md:91` — the `<a>`-tag rule
Rework: state each rule once in the nearest common ancestor; child docs inherit.

#### Rejected
- None — no auditor finding was rejected outright. Partial rejections folded into the adjusted rows above: #6's "Radix UI primitives is wrong" axis (Radix is genuinely a dependency); #9's quoted sentence "That was previously skipped…" (not present in `AGENTS.md`); #23's "`deepLinkWithFallback.ts` does not exist" (stale path, file is under `utils/browser/`); #28's "14+ orchestrators is wrong" (count is defensible; only the subfolder list is wrong); #29 kept as a phrasing nit since both statements are correct.

#### Checked and clean
- `apps/wallet/tests/README.md` — best prose in the repo; every "Gotcha" is a real trap, `TARGET_ENV`/Projects tables earn their cost (only nit is DM-31).
- `apps/wallet/AGENTS.md`, `apps/shopify/AGENTS.md`, `plugins/prestashop/AGENTS.md` — non-obvious, verifiable bullets; shopify's self-aware "README lies" note is honest.
- `infra/AGENTS.md`, `services/bootstrap/AGENTS.md`, `packages/app-essentials/AGENTS.md`, `sdk/components/AGENTS.md` — no count claims, no directory restatement, no dangling paths.
- `docs/audits/2026-08-05-frontend-findings-ranked-by-gain.md` and `-resolved.md` — self-correcting and mutation-verified; §0 demotes ten of the source audit's own headline numbers.
- `sdk/ios/CHANGELOG.md` and `plugins/magento/CHANGELOG.md` — hand-written Keep-a-Changelog, each entry naming a real failure mode or release constraint.
- Root `AGENTS.md` tooling claims verified (all four scripts exist, `package.json` chains them, `.changeset/config.json` ignore list correct, "4 apps, 8 packages, 3 plugins, 3 services" correct); `.opencode/instructions/*` and `opencode.json` short and accurate.

## 7. Artefacts

Raw auditor reports, verifier reports and merged sections were produced in `/tmp/slop-audit/{raw,verified,final}/` (not committed). This document is the only deliverable; no file under the repo was modified.
